## 1. Fixed bootstrap password enables remote administrator takeover

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/98d48d32ed248191afb4289b16f8a974?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=critical)

关闭
创建 Pull Request
Fixed bootstrap password enables remote administrator takeover
严重级别
Critical

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
7103440

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
补丁

概要

Introduced. Before this range, fresh administrators received a cryptographically random 24-byte base64url password, and legacy `admin123` credentials were automatically rotated to a random password. This commit replaces those protections with a fixed, documented password and additionally converts all existing administrators still in the forced-change state—including installations that previously had random bootstrap passwords—to `admin123` during startup.

The changed bootstrap logic hashes the constant `admin123` for every newly created SQLite or MariaDB administrator. It also resets any existing administrator whose `password_change_required` flag remains set to that same known password on every startup. An unauthenticated network attacker can therefore send one login request using `admin`/`admin123`, receive an authenticated administrator session, and call the change-password endpoint with `admin123` as the old password and an attacker-selected new password. The forced-password-change gate only blocks ordinary business APIs; authentication and password-change routes are deliberately mounted before that gate, so it does not prevent account takeover. Login throttling is ineffective against a correct password on the first attempt. Although the Node service binds to loopback, the stated deployment model exposes it through reverse proxies or tunnels, making the fresh-install or post-restore race remotely exploitable. Successful exploitation gives full administrator control over users, credentials, examinations, grades, sensitive student records, backups, and system configuration.

验证
 Confirm fixed admin/admin123 is provisioned on a fresh production-like SQLite initialization and the stored hash verifies.
 Confirm an unauthenticated request authenticates with that credential and obtains an admin session despite the forced-change flag and login throttles.
 Confirm the forced-change session can invoke the real password-change route with admin123, select a replacement password, and clear the gate.
 Confirm the resulting account has administrator authorization beyond the forced-change gate by reaching admin-only /api/users.
 Confirm realistic network reachability and document preconditions/countercontrols through the repository's Cloudflare Tunnel and Nginx deployment guidance.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Mounts authentication routes before the forced-password-change gate, allowing a holder of the bootstrap password to invoke the password-change endpoint.
- Defines the bootstrap administrator password as the fixed, publicly known value `admin123`.
- Resets every existing administrator still marked for forced password change to `admin123` at startup, exposing pending and restored installations.
- Creates both MariaDB and SQLite bootstrap administrators using the fixed password.
- The public login route accepts the known administrator credentials and creates an authenticated cookie or bearer token.
- After verifying `admin123` as the old password, an attacker can replace it with a chosen password, clear the forced-change flag, and obtain unrestricted administrator access by logging in again.

### [src/server/routes/auth.ts:83](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/7103440c483faab2974db72d8c3a29cdf366376b/src/server/routes/auth.ts#L83)

~~~~typescript
router.post("/login", loginIpLimiter, loginAccountLimiter, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { identifier, password, isPersistent } = req.body ?? {};

    if (typeof identifier !== "string" || typeof password !== "string" || !identifier || !password) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: "请输入用户名和密码" });
      return;
    }

    const result = await authService.login(identifier, password, !!isPersistent);

    if (!result.success) {
      res.status(401).json({ message: result.message });
      return;
    }

    if (result.token) {
      setAuthCookie(req, res, result.token, !!isPersistent);
    }

    // 安全审计（P1）：同源浏览器不返回 token（主通道 = HttpOnly Cookie）；
    // 跨源/脚本客户端才回传，避免同源 XSS 从响应体中窃取长期令牌。
    const includeToken = shouldReturnTokenInBody(req);
    res.json({
      ...(includeToken ? { token: result.token } : {}),
      httpOnlyAuth: true,
      user: result.user,
      permissions: result.permissions,
      passwordChangeRequired: result.passwordChangeRequired ?? false,
      message: result.message,
~~~~

攻击路径分析

Hard-suppression checks do not apply: the impact is not self-only; the attacker begins unauthenticated; no privileged, developer, physical-access, or protected-write-path prerequisite exists; and the documented public deployment workflow makes the preconditions plausible. Repository evidence and the executable PoC establish a short remote chain from public login to persistent administrator takeover. Impact is high because the resulting identity has wildcard permission over sensitive school data and administrative operations. Likelihood is high because admin/admin123 is fixed and publicly documented, a correct first attempt bypasses the practical value of throttling, and the forced-change endpoint is intentionally reachable before the gate. Applying the required matrix mechanically, high impact plus high likelihood remains critical because the threat model's critical criterion of unauthenticated administrator takeover is directly satisfied.

路径
Unauthenticated remote attacker --Reaches documented public deployment ingress--> Documented reverse proxy or Cloudflare tunnel --Forwards HTTPS traffic to loopback port 5174--> Public POST /api/auth/login --Correct first login issues a random session token--> Authenticated admin session with forced-change flag --Authentication routes precede the forced-change gate--> Pre-gate POST /api/auth/change-password --Known old password authorizes replacement and clears the flag--> Attacker-controlled administrator credential --Relogin obtains unrestricted admin wildcard permission--> Wildcard administrator access to protected data and operations

Attacker story: an unauthenticated Internet user discovers a Project-X endpoint exposed using the repository's documented Cloudflare tunnel or Nginx workflow. On a fresh, restored, or still-pending installation, startup creates or resets the admin account to the repository-documented password admin123. One correct login request yields an administrator session. The forced-change control returns 428 for normal APIs, but authentication routes, including change-password, are deliberately mounted before that gate. The attacker therefore changes the password using admin123 as the old value, clears the forced-change flag, and logs in again with unrestricted administrator permissions. The supplied executable PoC confirmed this complete chain through the real Express createApp() interface, including successful access to the protected user-list API after takeover. The credential is bcrypt-hashed in the database and also written to a mode-0600 bootstrap file, but those storage controls do not compensate for the same plaintext value being hard-coded and publicly documented. Login throttling permits a correct first attempt. The service's loopback bind, omission of the live tunnel URL, forced-change middleware, and protection of already-initialized admins are meaningful counterevidence and constrain affected deployments, but none prevents takeover during the explicitly supported public bootstrap or restore window.

发生可能性
High - Exploitation is remote under the repository's documented public tunnel and reverse-proxy workflows, requires no prior account or user interaction, and succeeds with one correct request using credentials published in the repository. The complete takeover path was exercised against the real Express application. Likelihood is constrained to fresh, restored, or forced-change deployments and ends after legitimate initialization, but that bootstrap race is a normal documented production workflow rather than an operator-only or contrived state. | Remote network vector
影响程度
High - The issue crosses the unauthenticated-to-administrator identity boundary. The admin role has wildcard permission, and repository routes expose user and credential management, scores and examinations, exports, scanner/API-key administration, permissions, and full backup/restore. This supports high confidentiality, integrity, and availability impact for educational records and the installation.
假设条件
The target is a fresh installation, a restored installation, or an existing installation whose admin account still has password_change_required=1.
The attacker can reach the documented reverse proxy, Cloudflare tunnel, or same-host API endpoint before the legitimate administrator changes the bootstrap password.
The deployed code corresponds to commit 7103440c483faab2974db72d8c3a29cdf366376b; the supplied executable PoC exercised SQLite, while MariaDB equivalence is established statically.
A fresh admin account or an existing/restored admin account with password_change_required=1
Network reachability to the Express API through localhost, the documented reverse proxy, or the documented Cloudflare tunnel
Exploitation before a legitimate administrator completes the initial password change
控制措施
Passwords are stored as bcrypt hashes rather than plaintext database values.
The bootstrap password file is created with mode 0600 where supported.
Login is limited to 10 attempts per account and 60 attempts per IP per 15 minutes.
Authentication uses random opaque session tokens.
HttpOnly and SameSite=Lax cookies are used for browser authentication.
requirePasswordChangeCompleted blocks ordinary APIs while password_change_required is set.
Password changes revoke the user's other sessions.
Existing administrators with password_change_required=0 are not reset.
The Node process binds to 127.0.0.1, requiring local access or a proxy/tunnel.
Downstream administration routes require authentication and permissions, but the compromised admin identity has wildcard permission.
盲点
Repository evidence establishes supported public ingress but cannot show whether any particular live installation currently has a reachable tunnel or reverse proxy.
The live random Cloudflare tunnel hostname is intentionally omitted, so attacker discovery of a specific quick-tunnel deployment is not established by the repository.
The executable PoC exercised SQLite; the corresponding MariaDB create and reset branches were verified statically but not dynamically.
The PoC proved admin takeover and access to an administrator-only user-list endpoint, but did not destructively invoke every downstream administrative operation.
An already-initialized administrator with password_change_required=0 is not vulnerable through this bootstrap path.

## 2. Login-page guide exposes bootstrap administrator credentials

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/134ccdfba8288191aae2a064c13c55f8?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=)

概要

Introduced: the range adds the user-guide entry point to the unauthenticated LoginPage and adds a bundled guide containing the live bootstrap administrator credentials. The guide should not expose credentials before authentication, and the bootstrap process should use a per-installation random one-time secret or local activation mechanism rather than a fixed password.

When no user is authenticated, App renders LoginPage. This commit adds a login-page button that opens UserGuideModal without authentication. UserGuidePage statically imports the full Markdown guide into the client bundle and renders it, while the guide explicitly states that the bootstrap administrator username is `admin` and the password is `admin123`. The backend creates that exact active administrator account, and successful login produces an administrator session; the administrator role has wildcard permissions. Therefore, an unauthenticated network attacker can visit an exposed installation, read the credentials directly from its login page, and authenticate before the legitimate operator changes the password. Any forced password-change flow does not protect against this race because the attacker can complete it first. This permits access to student records, papers, backups, credentials, and grade-management operations. Established deployments whose bootstrap password has already been changed are not affected. Although the fixed bootstrap credential predates this range, publishing it through the deployed pre-authentication UI is newly introduced by this commit.

验证
 Confirm unauthenticated production UI exposes the newly bundled guide and its literal admin / admin123 credential: verified in source, commit diff, successful Vite build, and unauthenticated HTTP-fetched bundle.
 Confirm a fresh supported database creates an active administrator whose effective password is admin123, without an activation prerequisite: verified from the fresh database and successful login.
 Exercise the real login interface and prove the disclosed credential yields an authenticated administrator session: production /api/auth/login returned HTTP 200, a token, admin identity, and wildcard permissions.
 Confirm administrator authorization and assess password-change controls: token accessed protected /api/users; attacker changed the password and then authenticated with the replacement password.
 Establish preconditions and introduction: commit diff proves pre-auth UI exposure was newly added, while evidence identifies the required fresh/restored unchanged account and deployment-dependent network reachability.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Unauthenticated visitors are rendered the LoginPage containing the new guide entry point.
- Adds a pre-authentication button that opens UserGuideModal without any authorization check.
- Imports the complete guide as raw build-time content and renders it into the client, making its credential text available in the public bundle and modal.
- The administrator role receives wildcard permission, establishing the complete confidentiality, integrity, and availability impact of successful takeover.
- Confirms that fresh databases create an active administrator using exactly the credentials disclosed by the guide.
- The login service accepts the disclosed credentials and returns a valid token and the administrator's permissions.
- The newly added guide explicitly publishes the live bootstrap administrator username and password.

### [src/server/db/index.ts:348](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/535ccbe86fddb40c815a85bd78bfc42c7b08f807/src/server/db/index.ts#L348)

~~~~typescript
export async function ensureDefaultAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  const db = getDatabase();
  const existing = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?").get("admin");
  if (existing) {
    return;
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword("admin123");
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, name, role_id, is_active)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run("admin", passwordHash, "系统管理员", 1, 1);
  console.log("[DB] Default admin created: username=admin, password=admin123");
~~~~

### [src/server/services/AuthService.ts:91](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/535ccbe86fddb40c815a85bd78bfc42c7b08f807/src/server/services/AuthService.ts#L91)

~~~~typescript
  async login(identifier: string, password: string, isPersistent = false): Promise<LoginResult> {
    // 查找用户（支持 username 或 student_number 或 email）
    let user = this.userRepo.findByUsername(identifier);

    if (!user && /^\d+$/.test(identifier)) {
      // 纯数字，尝试学号
      user = this.userRepo.findByStudentNumber(identifier);
    }

    if (!user) {
      return { success: false, message: "用户名或密码错误" };
    }

    // 验证密码。兼容旧流程留下的学生空密码哈希：首次用学号登录时自动补齐为学号密码。
    let valid = false;
    if (!user.password_hash && user.role_name === "student" && user.student_number && password === user.student_number) {
      const newHash = await hashPassword(user.student_number);
      const db = getDatabase();
      db.prepare("UPDATE users SET password_hash = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?").run(newHash, user.id);
      user.password_hash = newHash;
      valid = true;
    } else {
      valid = await verifyPassword(password, user.password_hash);
    }
    if (!valid) {
      return { success: false, message: "用户名或密码错误" };
    }

    // 更新最后登录时间
    this.userRepo.updateLastLogin(user.id);

    // 生成 token
    const token = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const expiresAt = Date.now() + (isPersistent ? PERSISTENT_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MS : TOKEN_EXPIRE_MS);
    this.tokenStore.set(token, { userId: user.id, expiresAt });
    this.scheduleSave();

    // 清除敏感信息
    const { password_hash, ...safeUser } = user;

    return {
      success: true,
      user: safeUser,
      token,
      permissions: permissionsForRole(user.role_id),
      message: "登录成功"
    };
~~~~

~~~~markdown
### 2.1 首次登录（管理员）

系统首次安装后自带默认管理员：
~~~~

攻击路径分析

Policy adjustment was applied after establishing the attack-path facts. Impact is high because the path crosses from unauthenticated access to an administrator identity with wildcard permissions and demonstrated protected-route and password-change capability. Likelihood is high for the affected state because the credential is delivered by the normal pre-authentication product UI, the login route is public, the documented production architecture supports remote access through Nginx, and an executable PoC completed the takeover path without speculative pivots. The fresh/restored and unchanged-password requirements narrow the affected population but are realistic deployment preconditions rather than privileged, operator-only, physical-access, or unachievable requirements. This is not self-only impact and no hard-suppression condition applies. Under the required matrix, high impact plus high likelihood remains critical because the direct unauthenticated administrator-takeover path meets the stated critical criteria.

路径
Unauthenticated remote attacker --Visits an exposed installation without authentication--> Public LoginPage and user-guide modal --Opens the guide, or directly downloads its public JavaScript asset--> Built client asset containing bootstrap credential documentation --Obtains and submits the documented fixed bootstrap credential--> POST /api/auth/login --Password verification succeeds and returns a session token--> Active bootstrap administrator with wildcard permissions --Wildcard permission satisfies protected route authorization--> Protected administrative and educational-data operations

Attacker story: an unauthenticated network user visits a newly deployed or restored Project-X instance before its operator changes the bootstrap administrator password. App renders LoginPage, which now exposes a guide modal before authentication. UserGuidePage imports the complete guide into the public client bundle, and the guide identifies the live fixed bootstrap administrator credential. The server independently creates an active administrator with that same credential. Submitting it to the ordinary login route produces a token whose role has wildcard permissions. Executable validation confirmed that the built unauthenticated asset contained the credential documentation, login succeeded, a protected administrator endpoint became accessible, and the attacker could change the administrator password and subsequently authenticate with the replacement password. The strongest counterevidence is that startServer binds 127.0.0.1, exploitation only applies before the default is changed, and the credential already appeared in repository documentation before this commit. These points narrow exposure and the affected deployment window but are not dispositive: deploy-guide.md explicitly recommends a public HTTPS Nginx proxy to the loopback port, fresh-install initialization automatically creates the active credential, no forced activation state blocks privileged operations, and this commit newly delivers the credential through the deployed pre-authentication product UI. Established installations with a changed password are not affected by this path.

发生可能性
High - For a reachable fresh or restored installation, exploitation is deterministic, requires no prior account or user interaction, and uses the normal client and login API. The repository documents public HTTPS reverse-proxy deployment, and executable validation confirmed the complete path through public bundle disclosure, successful login, wildcard permissions, protected endpoint access, and password replacement. Likelihood is constrained to the window before an operator changes the bootstrap password, so established installations with a rotated password are unaffected; nevertheless, the deployment race is plausible and directly identified by the project threat model. | Remote network vector
影响程度
High - Successful exploitation yields the installation's administrator identity with wildcard permissions. The executable validation proved access to a protected administrator endpoint and the ability to replace the administrator password. In the repository threat model, administrator compromise permits broad confidentiality and integrity impact over student records, accounts, exams, scores, backups, and system configuration, with potential destructive availability impact.
假设条件
The deployed client and API correspond to the affected commit.
The installation is fresh or restored and the bootstrap administrator password has not yet been changed.
The service is reachable through the repository-documented HTTPS reverse-proxy deployment or an equivalent supported ingress.
A fresh or restored database on which the fixed bootstrap administrator credential is still active
Network reachability to the web client and login API
The attacker authenticates before the legitimate operator changes the bootstrap password
控制措施
The Node service binds to 127.0.0.1 by default, requiring a local client or configured reverse proxy for remote reachability.
The login service performs bcrypt password verification and returns generic authentication failures.
Protected routes use authentication middleware and role/permission checks; anonymous requests to user-management routes are rejected.
Sessions use random opaque tokens, and browser cookies are HttpOnly with SameSite=Lax.
Changing a password revokes the user's existing sessions.
The guide tells operators to change the default password immediately.
These controls do not mitigate an attacker possessing the currently valid bootstrap credential; no pre-authentication activation or forced-password-change state blocks the first privileged session.
盲点
The checkout does not establish whether any particular real deployment currently exposes port 5174 through a proxy or tunnel; public exposure is supported and documented rather than proven for every installation.
The full createApp process could not be started in validation because the installed node_modules omitted a declared dependency; the executable harness instead used the repository's production initializer, authentication service, middleware, routers, built client, and a loopback HTTP server.
The validation proved a protected user-management operation and password replacement but did not execute every possible grading, backup, deletion, or configuration operation.
The fixed bootstrap credential predates this commit and was already present in repository documentation; the commit-specific regression is deployment of that credential in the unauthenticated client UI and public bundle.
Actual exploitability ends once the administrator password is changed, and repository evidence cannot determine how quickly individual operators perform that step.

## 3. Scanner payload can inject arbitrary subjective scores

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/a8d0c566afc8819187ec02f8260b6bc6?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=)

关闭
创建 Pull Request
Scanner payload can inject arbitrary subjective scores
严重级别
High

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
96dc520
9:27 PM Sep 19, 2026

操作者：Toyama Kasumi

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
补丁
聊天
概要

Introduced: before this range, the remote scanner endpoint uploaded images but did not accept and persist client-supplied recognition results as final grades. This commit adds recognition ingestion and automatic persistence without authoritative validation of subjective question identity or maximum score.

The commit adds client-provided recognition data to the remote scanner upload API. Its Zod schema only requires subjective scores and maximum scores to be finite and nonnegative; it does not require `questionId` or `questionNumber` to exist on the selected card. The payload is stored verbatim and later passed to `gradeSessionStudentResults`. Although objective questions are regraded against authoritative card definitions, `gradeSubjectiveRecognition` falls back to the attacker-provided `maxScore` whenever `questionId` is unknown and includes the supplied score in the student's total. The resulting total and invented question rows are then persisted to `student_scores` and `question_scores`. Consequently, an attacker possessing a scanner API key can create a session for a card associated with a single active exam, upload any valid image together with a payload such as an unknown subjective question with `score` and `maxScore` set to an arbitrary value, and complete the session to overwrite a roster student's grade. The same issue is reachable by a teacher with `GRADE_WRITE` and sufficient exam scope. The server should reject subjective entries not present in the card, derive question number and maximum score exclusively from the card, enforce uniqueness and authoritative coverage, and never use client-provided maxima as a fallback in this remote ingestion path.

验证
 Attacker-controlled remote recognition input is accepted without binding subjective IDs, numbers, or maxima to the selected card.
 An unknown subjective question uses the client-provided score/maxScore and contributes to aggregate grading totals.
 Session completion writes the forged total and invented question row for an eligible active exam and roster student.
 Authentication, exam resolution, roster, page-completeness, and duplicate/session controls do not prevent this path under the claimed scanner-key preconditions.
 A focused reproduction through the real HTTP interface demonstrates final database impact.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Stored client recognition data is parsed and passed into the grading logic without an authoritative subjective-question coverage check.
- The new recognition schema accepts arbitrary subjective question IDs, question numbers, scores, and client-controlled maximum scores without validating them against the answer card.
- The untrusted subjective recognition array is serialized directly into the database for later grading.
- The forged subjective amount is included in the persisted total, and invented subjective question rows are written to question_scores.
- For an unknown subjective question ID, grading trusts the recognition payload's maxScore and caps the attacker-provided score against that attacker-provided value.

### [src/shared/grading.ts:293](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/96dc520d9008ea606e1424cc2a5c8551ee9ae810/src/shared/grading.ts#L293)

~~~~typescript
export function gradeSubjectiveRecognition(
  card: AnswerCard,
  recognition: SubjectiveRecognitionQuestion
): SubjectiveQuestionGrade {
  const question = findSubjectiveQuestion(card, recognition.questionId);
  const maxScore = question?.score ?? recognition.maxScore;
  const needsReview = recognition.status !== "ok";

  let status: SubjectiveQuestionGradeStatus = "ok";
  if (recognition.status === "invalid") status = "invalid";
  else if (needsReview) status = "missing_score_grid";

  return {
    questionId: recognition.questionId,
    questionNumber: recognition.questionNumber,
    score: Math.max(0, Math.min(recognition.score, maxScore)),
    maxScore,
~~~~


攻击路径分析

Impact is high because arbitrary attacker-defined totals and invented question rows are persisted as authoritative grade data for another user. Likelihood is high for the relevant in-scope attacker because the path is remotely invocable in documented scanner-server mode, requires only an expected scanner machine credential, has low attack complexity, and was reproduced end to end. The hard suppression rules do not apply: impact is not self-only; preconditions are plausible; the path is not developer-, physical-access-, or host-operator-only; and the privilege delta is the core issue—a scanner identity permitted to submit OCR results can redefine authoritative question identities and maxima rather than remaining bounded by the card. The strongest counterevidence—feature flag disabled by default, loopback binding, scoped authentication, complete-page checks, exam resolution, and roster enforcement—reduces deployment prevalence but does not block the demonstrated path in the intended remote-scanner production workflow. Applying the required matrix mechanically, high impact plus high likelihood yields high rather than critical because the issue requires a valid scoped credential and affects data integrity without establishing installation-wide compromise or code execution.

路径
Malicious or compromised scanner API-key client --Authenticates with X-Api-Key and supplies valid image plus invented subjective score/maxScore--> Remote scanner session and multipart page upload API --Schema checks numeric shape but not authoritative card membership; payload is serialized verbatim--> twain_recognition_results containing client recognition JSON --Stored JSON is parsed and passed to grading--> gradeSessionStudentResults and gradeSubjectiveRecognition --Unknown question uses attacker maxScore and contributes to aggregate total--> Scanner result persistence transaction --Upserts forged aggregate and invented question row--> Forged student_scores and question_scores records

Attacker story: an attacker controlling a provisioned or compromised scanner API key uses the documented remote-scanner API to create a session for a card tied to one active exam. They upload a valid image while supplying an invented subjective entry such as questionId=invented-question-id, questionNumber=FORGED-999, score=777777, and maxScore=777777, then complete the session. The request schema verifies only basic shape and finite nonnegative numbers, stores the subjective array verbatim, and later passes it into grading. Because gradeSubjectiveRecognition falls back to the supplied maxScore for an unknown question ID, the invented score contributes to the aggregate and is persisted to student_scores and question_scores. The executable HTTP PoC observed completion status 200 and persisted a total of 777777 despite the authoritative card containing only a different question with a maximum of 10. The strongest counterevidence is substantial: the remote API is disabled by default, the process directly binds only to loopback, a scanner/full API key or GRADE_WRITE user is required, keys are scope-checked and normally hashed at rest, and completion checks tokens, images, page coverage, unique exam resolution, student existence, and roster membership. These controls are not dispositive because remote scanner mode is an expressly documented production workflow, repository comments anticipate proxy/tunnel ingress, and the PoC satisfied the authentication, session, image, exam, and roster controls. Although the scanner role is already trusted to submit OCR-derived grades, redefining the exam's question schema and maximum score is a demonstrated privilege and trust-boundary delta. The alternate teacher path is not needed to establish the vulnerability.

发生可能性
High - Exploitation is remote and low-complexity once the documented scanner workflow is enabled and a scanner/full key is controlled. It requires no race, victim interaction, parser exploit, or speculative second vulnerability, and the executable PoC traversed the real HTTP routes and persistence layer. Likelihood is constrained by the feature being opt-in, direct loopback binding, and the requirement for a valid machine credential plus a valid exam/student/page context. Nevertheless, scanner keys are expected production identities for remote scanner workstations, and all remaining preconditions are normal workflow state rather than highly unrealistic conditions. | Remote network vector
影响程度
High - The attacker can overwrite an existing roster student's authoritative aggregate grade with an arbitrary finite nonnegative value and introduce fabricated question-level records. These records feed academic results, ranking recomputation, and downstream analysis. The impact is not self-only and can be repeated for additional reachable students and active exams. This is a major integrity violation of a primary threat-model asset, although it does not directly provide code execution, confidentiality loss, or administrator takeover.
假设条件
The affected deployment has enabled the documented remote scanner workflow with PROJECTX_ENABLE_SCANNER_CLIENT_API=1; the repository does not establish that this optional mode is enabled in every deployment.
The attacker controls a legitimately provisioned or compromised scanner/full API key. No separate API-key theft mechanism is claimed.
The selected answer card resolves to exactly one eligible active exam, and the supplied student number identifies an existing student who is in the exam roster when roster enforcement applies.
The alternate authenticated-teacher path was not dynamically exercised, so the scanner API-key path is the primary basis for reachability and severity.
Remote scanner client API enabled through PROJECTX_ENABLE_SCANNER_CLIENT_API=1
Valid scanner/full API key, or an authenticated user with GRADE_WRITE and sufficient whole-card exam scope
Answer card associated with exactly one eligible active exam
Existing target student who is an exam participant when a roster is known
Valid image, session upload token, complete page layout, and recognition data for every uploaded page
控制措施
Remote scanner routes are disabled unless PROJECTX_ENABLE_SCANNER_CLIENT_API is enabled.
The Node process binds directly to 127.0.0.1; remote access requires an operator-provided proxy, tunnel, or equivalent forwarding.
dualAuth requires a valid scanner/full API key or an authenticated user with GRADE_WRITE.
API keys are checked for active status and scanner/full scope, and newly issued keys are stored as SHA-256 hashes.
Teacher-authenticated writes require exam access and whole-card grading scope.
Session upload tokens must correspond to records in the selected session.
The uploaded file must pass image magic-byte validation.
Completion requires all expected pages, recognition data, and a resolvable single exam.
Persistence requires an existing student and enforces roster membership when the exam roster is known.
Grade and question writes occur in a transaction.
None of these controls verifies subjective question identity, authoritative question number, authoritative maximum score, uniqueness, or coverage.
盲点
No deployment manifests or reverse-proxy configuration establish whether a particular installation exposes port 5174 through a LAN, public tunnel, or Internet-facing domain.
The repository cannot establish whether PROJECTX_ENABLE_SCANNER_CLIENT_API is enabled in a specific production deployment.
The executable PoC used SQLite; the vulnerable grading logic is database-independent, but MariaDB persistence was not separately exercised.
The authenticated GRADE_WRITE teacher path was not dynamically reproduced.
No independent API-key acquisition or workstation-compromise chain is claimed.
The analysis confirms one-student persistence per demonstrated session; large-scale exploitation would require repeating the normal scanner workflow for additional students or exams.

## 4. PDF rendering enables authenticated sidecar resource exhaustion

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/8abd069173b88191a6aa18aebf56ebfd?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=)

关闭
创建 Pull Request
PDF rendering enables authenticated sidecar resource exhaustion
严重级别
High

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
20e4e08
1:33 PM Aug 31, 2026

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
补丁
聊天
概要

This commit introduces the vulnerable local parser sink. Previously the direct multimodal path forwarded uploaded files to the configured provider; it now unconditionally calls normalize_direct_files, which decodes and renders PDFs inside the Project-X sidecar without resource isolation or complexity limits.

A teacher with the normal grading permission can upload a PDF and invoke the knowledge-point analysis endpoint when a vision-capable provider is configured. Upload validation limits each file to 50 MB and checks its filename extension, but does not validate PDF magic or constrain compressed stream sizes, object counts, page complexity, or aggregate request size. The newly introduced normalize_direct_files path base64-decodes each PDF and synchronously opens and renders every page with PyMuPDF. The 40-page limit and 2048-pixel output target do not bound the work required to decompress and interpret a page's content stream; a one-page PDF can contain highly compressed or pathologically complex graphics. The implementation also retains every rendered JPEG as another base64 string until all pages finish. Because the FastAPI handler is synchronous, aborting the Node request after 60 seconds does not reliably interrupt the ongoing native rendering operation. Repeated analysis requests can therefore consume the sidecar's worker threads, CPU, and memory and disrupt AI functionality or the host during grading operations. The conversion should run in a resource-limited subprocess with a hard execution timeout, enforce PDF magic and aggregate input/decompressed-content limits, and rate-limit analysis requests.

验证
 Reachability/control: a default non-admin teacher has the permission needed to upload an accepted PDF and invoke the multimodal knowledge-point path when a vision provider is configured.
 Data flow/sink: stored attacker-controlled PDF bytes are base64-forwarded to normalize_direct_files and synchronously rendered in-process by PyMuPDF before provider egress.
 Missing effective controls: page count, DPI, and output dimensions do not bound content-stream drawing complexity, rendering CPU time, or execution with a hard isolated timeout.
 Realistic impact: a valid 940-byte, one-page PDF reproducibly consumed 64.643 seconds through the real normalization implementation, exceeding the Node caller's 60-second timeout.
 Operational persistence: real HTTP tests showed rendering continued after client cancellation, and three concurrent disconnected requests remained active in separate sidecar threads without an analysis-specific rate/concurrency limit.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Attacker-influenced PDF bytes are opened and every permitted page is synchronously rendered in-process; only page count and output DPI are bounded, not decompressed stream complexity, execution time, or memory.
- Every PDF is fully base64-decoded, and all rendered page images are accumulated as base64 strings with no aggregate byte limit.
- The changed direct multimodal path now unconditionally normalizes files, introducing local PDF decoding and rendering before the provider call.
- The upload-side validator allows files based on extension and a 50 MB per-file limit, without PDF content validation or decompression-complexity controls.
- An authenticated knowledge-point request forwards the stored PDF to the synchronous sidecar conversion path; the 60-second client timeout is not a hard execution limit for rendering.

### [llmclient/pdf_to_images.py:25](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/20e4e085ec293c3f27f6d6ae7c6af5be63015681/llmclient/pdf_to_images.py#L25)

~~~~python
def pdf_bytes_to_images(pdf_bytes: bytes, max_pages: int = MAX_AI_PAGES) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """把一份 PDF 渲染成压缩 JPEG 页图（base64），每页长边 <= 2048。"""
    doc = pymupdf.open(stream=pdf_bytes, filetype="pdf")
    try:
        page_count = doc.page_count
        if page_count > max_pages:
            raise PdfPageLimitError(
                f"PDF 共 {page_count} 页，超过多模态直传上限 {max_pages} 页"
            )
        images: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        for page in doc:
            long_pt = max(page.rect.width, page.rect.height)
            dpi = min(MAX_DPI, int(LONG_EDGE * 72 / long_pt))
            pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=dpi)
            data = pix.tobytes("jpeg", jpg_quality=JPEG_QUALITY)
            images.append({"mimeType": "image/jpeg", "base64": base64.b64encode(data).decode()})
        return images
    finally:
        doc.close()
~~~~

### [llmclient/pdf_to_images.py:46](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/20e4e085ec293c3f27f6d6ae7c6af5be63015681/llmclient/pdf_to_images.py#L46)

~~~~python
def normalize_direct_files(files: list[dict[str, str]], max_pages: int = MAX_AI_PAGES) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """直传前归一化：PDF 转压缩页图，图片原样保留。"""
    out: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    pdf_pages_used = 0
    for f in files:
        mime = (f.get("mimeType") or "").lower()
        if mime == "application/pdf":
            page_images = pdf_bytes_to_images(
                base64.b64decode(f["base64"]),
                max_pages=max_pages - pdf_pages_used,
            )
            out.extend(page_images)
            pdf_pages_used += len(page_images)
        else:
            out.append(f)
    return out
~~~~

### [llmclient/providers_knowledge_points.py:103](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/20e4e085ec293c3f27f6d6ae7c6af5be63015681/llmclient/providers_knowledge_points.py#L103)

~~~~python
def run_direct_multimodal(
    model: ModelConfig,
    files: list[dict[str, str]],
    subject: str,
    question_range: str,
    extra_notes: str,
    answer_card_json: str = "",
    provider_override: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """直传图片给多模态模型 (Gemini / GPT)."""
    # PDF 原卷先转压缩页图（OpenAI 兼容接口不接受 PDF data URL）
    files = normalize_direct_files(files)
~~~~

~~~~typescript
export const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50MB

export function validatePaperFile(filename: string, size: number): string | null {
  const ext = path.extname(filename).toLowerCase();
  if (ext === ".doc") return "不支持 .doc 格式，请转为 .docx 后上传";
  if (!ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)) return `不支持 ${ext} 格式，请上传 DOCX/PDF/图片文件`;
  if (size > MAX_FILE_SIZE) return `文件过大（${(size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)}MB），最大 50MB`;
  return null;
~~~~

攻击路径分析

The established impact is high because executable validation demonstrates sustained shared CPU and worker consumption beyond client cancellation, matching the threat model's high-impact availability category for authenticated resource exhaustion during grading. Likelihood is high because a normal teacher with default GRADE_WRITE can remotely use the supported upload and analysis workflow, the malicious document can be valid and extremely small, and no render rate or concurrency control exists. Applying the required matrix mechanically, impact=high and likelihood=high produces high unless critical criteria are independently met. Critical is not warranted because the proven effect is confined to availability of one deployment, requires teacher authentication and vision-provider configuration, and does not establish RCE, data compromise, score corruption, or fleet-wide impact. No hard suppression applies: this is neither self-only nor operator/developer/physical-only, and the teacher's normal protected write permission is the intended lower-privilege workflow rather than an administrative precondition.

路径
Authenticated teacher with GRADE_WRITE --uploads a .pdf accepted by extension and per-file size checks--> Express POST /api/cards/:cardId/paper on port 5174 --copies the PDF unchanged into card paper storage--> Stored attacker-controlled valid PDF --route reads stored original*.pdf files into base64--> Express POST /api/cards/:cardId/knowledge-points/analyze --selects direct mode when the configured provider supports vision--> Node fetchLlmClient with 60-second AbortController timeout --forwards files with the internal sidecar bearer key--> Internal FastAPI /analysis/knowledge-points on localhost port 8766 --synchronous handler invokes normalization before provider egress--> normalize_direct_files and PyMuPDF page.get_pixmap --unbounded content-stream interpretation continues beyond client cancellation--> Persistent CPU and concurrent worker consumption

Attacker story: a malicious or compromised teacher uses the normal original-paper workflow to upload a valid, highly compressed PDF containing many expensive drawing operations. The teacher then starts knowledge-point analysis while a vision provider is configured. The card route reads the stored PDF into base64 and forwards it to the internal LLM sidecar. Before selecting or contacting the external provider, run_direct_multimodal calls normalize_direct_files, which decodes the complete PDF and synchronously renders every page with PyMuPDF. Page-count, DPI, and raster-edge limits do not bound the complexity of interpreting a page's content stream. The supplied executable validation used the repository's real implementation. A valid one-page 940-byte PDF with 10,000 repeated stroke operations took 64.643 seconds to normalize and reached approximately 85,844 KiB RSS. A request containing a 722-byte variant was sent to the actual FastAPI endpoint; after the client disconnected at one second, process CPU ticks increased from 268 to 775 during the next five seconds. Three disconnected concurrent requests left four server threads active and continued consuming CPU. This demonstrates a shared availability effect, not merely a slow response to the attacker. The strongest scoping counterevidence is that Node binds to 127.0.0.1, the sidecar defaults to 127.0.0.1:8766 and requires LLMCLIENT_INTERNAL_API_KEY, and the outer route is protected by GRADE_WRITE. These facts prevent unauthenticated direct sidecar access but are not dispositive: the supported Node product route intentionally bridges an authenticated teacher request into the sidecar, and the repository describes reverse-proxy or tunnel ingress for public traffic. The strongest technical countercontrols are the 50 MiB per-file limit, 40-page aggregate limit, 300 DPI ceiling, 2048-pixel long edge, and Node's 60-second AbortController timeout. They are not dispositive because the executable input was a valid sub-kilobyte, one-page PDF and the render continued after disconnection. The proof establishes sustained CPU and concurrent worker use, but not native code execution, secret disclosure, score corruption, a crash, or a complete host outage.

发生可能性
High - The path is a normal remote product workflow available to the default teacher role, requires no victim interaction, accepts a valid sub-kilobyte one-page PDF, and has no analysis rate or concurrency limit. Authentication, the need for an existing card, and a configured vision provider are meaningful but ordinary operational preconditions rather than unachievable privileged setup. Loopback sidecar binding does not prevent exploitation because Express intentionally forwards the request. | Remote network vector
影响程度
High - Repeated authenticated requests can continue consuming shared sidecar CPU and execution threads after callers disconnect, disrupting AI functionality during grading and potentially contending with other services on the same host. The executable proof confirms prolonged CPU and concurrent-worker occupancy. Impact is limited to availability: there is no evidence of code execution, credential disclosure, grade modification, or fleet-wide propagation.
假设条件
Production uses the repository's default-on authentication mode; PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE has not been explicitly disabled.
A legitimate or compromised teacher account has the default GRADE_WRITE permission and access to an answer card on which an original paper can be stored.
A vision-capable knowledge-point provider is configured, causing resolveKnowledgePointMode to select the direct multimodal path.
The deployment exposes the loopback-bound Node service through the reverse-proxy or tunnel deployment model identified by the repository and supplied threat model.
Observed PyMuPDF timing is hardware- and version-dependent, although the executable validation establishes that valid sub-kilobyte input can exceed the application's 60-second timeout.
An authenticated teacher session with GRADE_WRITE
An existing answer card on which the teacher can upload an original paper
A configured vision-capable AI provider
Upload of a valid pathologically complex PDF followed by a knowledge-point analysis request
Repeated or concurrent requests for broader service degradation
控制措施
Authentication is default-on and the outer POST routes require GRADE_WRITE.
The default sidecar address is localhost and /analysis/knowledge-points requires LLMCLIENT_INTERNAL_API_KEY.
Paper uploads are limited to 40 files and 50 MiB per file.
PDF conversion allows at most 40 aggregate PDF pages, at most 300 DPI, and a nominal 2048-pixel long raster edge.
The Node-to-sidecar request uses a 60-second AbortController timeout.
CORS is allowlisted, but CORS does not prevent a malicious authenticated API client.
Only login-specific rate limiting was identified; no analysis rate, queue, or render-concurrency control was found.
No hard subprocess timeout, CPU quota, memory limit, decompressed-content bound, or cancellation mechanism protects PyMuPDF rendering.
盲点
No deployment manifest proves that a particular installation has an Internet-facing reverse proxy or tunnel; exposure is supported by source comments and the supplied production threat model but remains deployment-dependent.
The executable validation exercised the real normalization function and authenticated FastAPI sidecar endpoint, but not a complete browser/Node/database/provider end-to-end deployment.
The number of requests needed for a complete sidecar or host outage depends on CPU capacity, Uvicorn/AnyIO thread-pool behavior, PyMuPDF version, and any external process or container limits not represented in the repository.
The proof measured sustained CPU and worker occupancy and moderate RSS, not a process crash, catastrophic memory exhaustion, or collateral failure of the Node server.
Whether a vision-capable provider and usable answer card exist is operational state not determinable from repository artifacts.
No repository IaC establishes host-level cgroups, service-manager limits, reverse-proxy request limits, or external WAF/rate controls.

## 5. Out-of-scope grading upload can unpublish any exam

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/2e411e2dc828819181445528bc2dadce?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=)

关闭
创建 Pull Request
Out-of-scope grading upload can unpublish any exam
严重级别
High

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
336964f
6:35 AM Aug 25, 2026

操作者：NaOH1g

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
补丁
聊天
概要

Introduced. The grading endpoint's missing exam-object authorization predates this range, but this commit adds the automatic score_published=0 transition before row validation. Consequently, even a completely failed or empty out-of-scope upload now performs a new unauthorized publication-state change and leaves it in place when the previous exam status is restored.

POST /api/cards/:cardId/grading is protected only by the generic card gate, which requires GRADE_WRITE for a POST request. It reads the target exam ID from the multipart body and passes it directly to persistGradingResults without calling requireExamAccess, checking getVisibleExamIds, or confirming that the exam is linked to :cardId. The changed persistence code now sets the selected exam to grading and changes score_published from 1 to 0 before determining whether any uploaded row was recognized or accepted. This makes exploitation particularly simple: for a known single-sided card, an attacker can upload one arbitrary file named with the B-side suffix so gradingFiles becomes empty, or use a malformed file that produces a failed recognition row. The batch then has status error and restores the previous exam status, but it does not restore score_published, leaving the victim exam unpublished. Because teacher roles have GRADE_WRITE by default and exam IDs are numeric, a subject or head teacher can repeatedly withdraw results for exams outside their permitted class, subject, or exam scope. The audit row records the actor, and an authorized teacher can republish the exam, but neither prevents scalable disruption of score publication. The route should authorize the body examId against the requesting user's exam visibility, verify exam.card_id equals the URL cardId, and avoid changing publication state until at least one authorized row will be persisted; failure rollback must also restore publication state.

验证
 Verify a normal authenticated teacher can reach the grading endpoint through the generic GRADE_WRITE card gate.
 Establish that the supplied exam is outside the attacker's normal requireExamAccess scope and is not linked to the URL card.
 Demonstrate attacker-controlled multipart input can create an empty or wholly failed grading batch while still passing examId to persistence.
 Verify score_published is changed before row acceptance and that the error path restores exam status but not publication state.
 Demonstrate durable user-visible impact: zero rows persist, an attacker-attributed audit event is written, and students lose access to the previously published score.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Persistence loads any numeric exam ID, changes it to grading, and newly withdraws published scores before validating whether any grading row can be persisted.
- On a failed or empty batch the previous exam status is restored, but score_published is not restored, so the unauthorized withdrawal persists.
- The /api/cards POST gate checks only the role-wide GRADE_WRITE permission; it does not establish access to the exam supplied later in the multipart body.
- The grading route accepts files and takes examId directly from req.body without checking exam visibility or linking the exam to :cardId.
- The attacker-controlled body examId is passed directly to persistGradingResults.
- GRADE_WRITE is granted to teacher accounts by default, making this reachable by ordinary authenticated teachers rather than only administrators.

~~~~typescript
  const examId = Number(examIdParam);

  if (!exam) {
    throw Object.assign(new Error("考试不存在"), { status: 404, code: ApiError.NOT_FOUND });
  }
  const previousExamStatus = exam.status;

  // 重新阅卷会逐学生改写 student_scores —— 已公布的考试先自动撤下（记审计），
  // 避免学生看到阅卷中途的半成品成绩（与 v41 公布门控配套）；阅卷完成结考后需教师重新公布。
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const txExamRepo = new ExamRepository(tx);
    await txExamRepo.updateStatus(examId, "grading");
    if (exam.score_published === 1) {
      await tx.run(
        "UPDATE exams SET score_published = 0, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ? AND score_published = 1",
        examId
      );
      await tx.run(
        "INSERT INTO exam_publish_events (exam_id, action, actor_id, reason) VALUES (?, 'unpublish', ?, ?)",
        examId, createdBy ?? null, "重新阅卷自动撤回"
      );
    }
  });
~~~~

### [src/server/auth/permissions.ts:66](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/336964f1e839dfd897ef2faaf16217e397c36777/src/server/auth/permissions.ts#L66)



攻击路径分析

No hard suppression applies: the impact is not self-only, the preconditions are plausible, and the attacker is a lower-privileged teacher crossing an object-scope boundary rather than an administrator exercising intended authority. Repository controls require authentication and GRADE_WRITE, but the privilege-escalation delta is precisely that these role-wide rights are converted into mutation authority over an exam that requireExamAccess denies. Impact is high because publication can be withdrawn across unrelated exams and student access is demonstrably lost; likelihood is high because the normal network endpoint accepts a simple body ID and filename-only trigger with no recognition success or speculative chain. Applying the required matrix mechanically, high impact multiplied by high likelihood yields high, not critical. Critical is not warranted because no scores are corrupted or disclosed, the action is audited, and authorized staff can restore publication.

路径
Authenticated teacher with default GRADE_WRITE --authenticated multipart request passes generic GRADE_WRITE gate--> POST /api/cards/:cardId/grading --supplies attacker-selected examId unrelated to :cardId--> Body examId accepted without exam-scope or card-link authorization --B-suffixed file is discarded for a single-sided card--> Single-sided card filters B-suffixed upload, producing rows=[] --empty rows are still passed to persistence--> persistGradingResults clears exams.score_published --error rollback restores status but not score_published--> Victim students can no longer view the exam's results

A realistic attacker is a legitimate subject teacher whose account has the default GRADE_WRITE capability but whose assigned subject and class do not include the victim exam. The teacher submits POST /api/cards/:cardId/grading using any accessible single-sided card, puts the victim's auto-incrementing exam ID in the multipart body, and uploads an arbitrary file named with the B-side suffix. The generic /api/cards gate admits the request based only on GRADE_WRITE. The handler filters out the B-side file, obtains an empty rows array, and passes the unvalidated body examId to persistGradingResults. Persistence clears score_published before checking rows; the resulting error path restores only exam.status. The supplied executable validation confirmed a 403 from the normal exam endpoint for the same teacher, followed by a 422 grading response that nevertheless changed the unrelated exam from published to unpublished and made it disappear from the victim student's score view. The audit row records the actor and an authorized teacher can republish, which limits stealth and permanence, but neither control prevents a teacher from repeatedly disrupting publication across scoped boundaries. The existing security test is counterevidence only for intended authorized behavior: it directly invokes persistence and asserts that empty-row regrading unpublishes an exam, but it does not exercise route-level exam authorization or card linkage.

发生可能性
High - Exploitation requires a legitimate teacher account, but teachers receive GRADE_WRITE by default and use this multipart endpoint as part of normal grading. The attacker controls examId and filename, needs no native-recognizer success, and can trigger the sink with one small B-suffixed upload. The executable HTTP validation demonstrated the complete path while normal exam access returned 403. Public Internet ingress is deployment-dependent, but the product is designed for teacher access through a school server or proxy, so no local host or operator privilege is required. | Remote network vector
影响程度
High - The attacker can cross an explicit class/subject/exam authorization boundary and durably change the publication state of unrelated exams. Students immediately lose access to affected results. Auto-incrementing IDs and the absence of per-route throttling make repeated disruption across multiple published exams plausible. The underlying scores are not changed, the action is audited, and an authorized teacher can republish, so this does not meet the threshold for critical integrity compromise; nevertheless, scalable disruption of official score publication during school operations is a major availability and workflow-integrity impact.
假设条件
The deployment makes the web application available to teacher browsers through the documented school-server, reverse-proxy, or tunnel deployment pattern; repository artifacts do not prove a specific live public ingress.
The attacker already has a legitimate authenticated teacher account with the default GRADE_WRITE capability; no credential theft or administrator access is assumed.
At least one victim exam is currently published, and the attacker can know or guess its auto-incrementing numeric exam ID.
A single-sided answer-card ID is available to the teacher. Cards are protected only by role-wide card permissions and are intentionally shared within an installation.
The executable validation used SQLite. The authorization omission, publication update, audit insertion, and incomplete rollback are in shared application code and are therefore expected to affect MariaDB as well.
A valid teacher session with GRADE_WRITE
A published victim exam outside the teacher's permitted class, subject, or exam scope
Knowledge or guessing of the victim's numeric exam ID
Access to any existing single-sided card ID
A multipart upload containing at least one file whose name matches the B-side suffix pattern
控制措施
Authentication enforcement is enabled by default; unauthenticated requests are rejected by makeGate.
The route requires the role-wide GRADE_WRITE capability, excluding students under the default permission mapping.
requireExamAccess and getVisibleExamIds provide a strong exam-scope control on normal exam routes, but are omitted from this route.
Multer limits each uploaded recognition file to 20 MiB.
The publication withdrawal is written to exam_publish_events with the actor ID and reason.
An authorized teacher can republish the exam after detecting the disruption.
The participant check prevents out-of-roster score rows from being persisted, but it runs after publication has already been withdrawn.
There is no route-level rate limit preventing repeated unpublication attempts.
CSP, CORS, and React escaping do not mitigate a valid authenticated direct API request.
盲点
No repository deployment manifest proves whether a specific installation is Internet-public, LAN-only, or exposed through a particular reverse proxy or Cloudflare tunnel.
The executable validation covered the SQLite deployment path, not a live MariaDB instance, although the vulnerable application flow and SQL operations are shared.
The repository does not establish how readily a teacher can learn every formal exam ID in a particular production dataset; IDs are auto-incrementing and therefore guessable, but failed guesses may generate operational evidence.
Operational alerting outside the repository could detect repeated audit events, but it would not prevent the unauthorized state changes.
The analysis does not claim score corruption through mismatched recognition data; the proven impact is unauthorized unpublication with zero accepted rows.

## 6. Quiz visibility grants every teacher destructive exam access

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/fb904f4d89b08191b6a0236883c663f6?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=)

关闭
创建 Pull Request
Quiz visibility grants every teacher destructive exam access
严重级别
High

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
7ef4296
6:21 PM Aug 7, 2026

操作者：Tim-Hanchuan-Yang

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
补丁
聊天
概要

Introduced an authorization vulnerability by merging all quiz exams into the generic visibility set without separating read visibility from write, grading, mode-management, and deletion authorization. Existing destructive routes immediately inherit the newly broadened scope.

The commit adds every `exam_mode = 'quiz'` exam to every scoped teacher's result from `getVisibleExamIds`, regardless of class, subject, or explicit restrictions. However, `requireExamAccess` treats membership in that visibility list as authorization for all HTTP methods, not merely reads. Default teacher roles possess both exam and grading write permissions, and several destructive routes use only this method-agnostic check. Consequently, a subject teacher or head teacher with no relationship to a quiz exam can enumerate it through the exam list and invoke endpoints such as `PUT /api/exams/:examId/student/:studentId/scores`, `PUT /api/exams/:examId/answers`, or `DELETE /api/exams/:examId`. The answer-key endpoint rewrites the associated card and automatically recomputes every student's objective score, while deletion removes question scores, total scores, scan batches, and the exam. The newly added mode update is similarly protected only by `requireExamAccess`, despite the client exposing that control only to administrators; a teacher can therefore change accessible formal exams to quiz and broaden their exposure, or change arbitrary quiz exams to formal. Quiz read visibility may be intentional, but using the same scope as mutation authorization crosses class, subject, and grading boundaries.

验证
 Confirmed a normal scoped teacher can authenticate and receives unrelated quiz IDs despite having no ownership, class assignment, or matching subject.
 Confirmed the capability gate and requireExamAccess do not distinguish read visibility from score, mode, or deletion operations.
 Exercised real mounted HTTP routes and verified consequential score, mode, exam, score-row, and scan-batch state changes.
 Used the production Express/auth middleware plus the repository's focused verify:176-178 harness; all assertions passed.
 Checked counterevidence: unrelated formal deletion is denied, custom roles could remove exam:write, and broad quiz visibility is documented, but no downstream ownership/admin/grading-scope check prevents the reproduced writes.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Exam deletion relies on the same broadened access check and deletes the exam's persisted rows without an ownership, class, subject, or administrator check.
- The new exam-mode update is accepted for any caller passing requireExamAccess, although the client presents mode switching as administrator-only.
- The newly added helper unconditionally merges every quiz exam into each scoped teacher's visible exam IDs.
- The generic access middleware treats visibility as authorization without distinguishing reads from score changes, mode changes, or deletion.
- A write endpoint guarded only by requireExamAccess changes question and total scores and recomputes rankings, so the broadened quiz scope permits cross-class score tampering.
- Another requireExamAccess-only endpoint changes the answer key on the associated card and begins recalculating scores for all participating students.

### [src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts:1815](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/7ef4296e7cbea2e2eb5d8129f5f9120f76c7ea8d/src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts#L1815)

~~~~typescript
  app.delete("/api/exams/:examId", requireExamAccess, async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const examRepo = new ExamRepository();
      const exam = await examRepo.findExamById(Number(req.params.examId));
      if (!exam) {
        res.status(404).json({ message: "考试不存在" });
        return;
      }
      const body = (req.body ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
      const deleteLinkedCard = requestFlag(body.deleteLinkedCard);
      const linkedCardId = exam.card_id ? safeId(exam.card_id) : null;
      const db = getMysqlDb();
      if (deleteLinkedCard && linkedCardId) {
        const referencedByOtherExams = (await examRepo.listExams()).filter((item) => item.card_id === linkedCardId && item.id !== exam.id);
        if (referencedByOtherExams.length > 0) {
          res.status(409).json({
            message: `无法同时删除答题卡：仍被 ${referencedByOtherExams.length} 个其它考试引用`,
            referencedExamCount: referencedByOtherExams.length,
            referencedExamNames: referencedByOtherExams.map((item) => item.name)
          });
          return;
        }
      }
      await deleteExamRows(db, [exam.id]);
~~~~

### [src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts:1851](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/7ef4296e7cbea2e2eb5d8129f5f9120f76c7ea8d/src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts#L1851)

~~~~typescript
  app.patch("/api/exams/:examId", requireExamAccess, async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const examRepo = new ExamRepository();
      const exam = await examRepo.findExamById(Number(req.params.examId));
      if (!exam) {
        res.status(404).json({ message: "考试不存在" });
        return;
      }
      const { cardId, name, subject, mode } = req.body as Record<string, unknown>;
      const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      if (cardId !== undefined) updates.card_id = String(cardId);
      if (name !== undefined) updates.name = String(name);
      if (subject !== undefined) updates.subject = String(subject);
      if (mode === "quiz" || mode === "formal") updates.exam_mode = mode;

      // Whitelist: only these columns may appear in a dynamic UPDATE
      const ALLOWED_COLUMNS = new Set(["updated_at", "card_id", "name", "subject", "exam_mode"]);
      for (const col of Object.keys(updates)) {
        if (!ALLOWED_COLUMNS.has(col)) {
          res.status(400).json({ message: `不支持的更新字段：${col}` });
          return;
        }
      }

      const { getMysqlDb } = await import("../../../server/db");
      const db = getMysqlDb();
      const setClauses = Object.keys(updates).map((k) => `${k} = ?`).join(", ");
      const values = Object.values(updates);
      await db.run(`UPDATE exams SET ${setClauses} WHERE id = ?`, ...values, exam.id);
~~~~

~~~~typescript
  async function withQuizExamIds(ids: number[]): Promise<number[]> {
    // 晨测模式 = 全量权限：无论 teacher_role / teacher_permissions 如何限制，quiz 考试都可见
    const quizRows = await db.all<{ id: number }>("SELECT id FROM exams WHERE exam_mode = 'quiz'");
    const merged = new Set(ids);
    for (const row of quizRows) merged.add(row.id);
    return Array.from(merged);
~~~~

~~~~typescript
router.put("/:examId/student/:studentId/scores", requireExamAccess, async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  const examId = Number(req.params.examId);
  const studentId = Number(req.params.studentId);
  if (!Number.isFinite(examId) || !Number.isFinite(studentId)) {
    res.status(400).json({ message: "非法考试或学生 ID" });
    return;
  }

  const updatesRaw = req.body?.scores;
  if (!Array.isArray(updatesRaw) || updatesRaw.length === 0) {
    res.status(400).json({ message: "未提供分数修改数据" });
    return;
  }
  // 逐元素校验，防止非法数据进入 SQL / 计分计算
  for (const u of updatesRaw) {
    if (
      !u || typeof u !== "object" ||
      typeof u.questionNumber !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(u.questionNumber) ||
      typeof u.scoreType !== "string" || u.scoreType.length === 0 ||
      typeof u.score !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(u.score)
    ) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: "分数修改数据格式非法" });
      return;
    }
  }
  const updates = updatesRaw as Array<{ questionNumber: number; scoreType: string; score: number }>;

  const db = getMysqlDb();
  const userId = req.user!.id;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const exam = await db.get("SELECT card_id FROM exams WHERE id = ?", examId) as { card_id: string | null } | undefined;
  if (!exam) { res.status(404).json({ message: "考试不存在" }); return; }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    for (const u of updates) {
      const existing = await tx.get(
        "SELECT id, score, max_score FROM question_scores WHERE exam_id = ? AND student_id = ? AND question_number = ? AND score_type = ?",
        examId, studentId, u.questionNumber, u.scoreType
      ) as { id: number; score: number; max_score: number } | undefined;

      if (existing) {
        // P1-7: 分数未变时不标记 manually_modified
        const scoreChanged = existing.score !== u.score;
        await tx.run(
          `UPDATE question_scores SET score = ?, manually_modified = ?, modified_by = ?, modified_at = ?
           WHERE exam_id = ? AND student_id = ? AND question_number = ? AND score_type = ?`,
          u.score, scoreChanged ? 1 : 0, userId, now, examId, studentId, u.questionNumber, u.scoreType
        );
        await tx.run(
          `INSERT INTO answer_overrides (exam_id, card_id, question_number, score_type, override_type, old_value, new_value, created_by, created_at)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'score', ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
          examId, exam.card_id, u.questionNumber, u.scoreType,
          JSON.stringify(existing.score), JSON.stringify(u.score), userId, now
        );
      }
    }

    const rows = await tx.all(
      "SELECT score, score_type FROM question_scores WHERE exam_id = ? AND student_id = ?",
      examId, studentId
    ) as any[];

    let totalObjective = 0, totalSubjective = 0;
    for (const s of rows) {
      if (s.score_type === "objective") totalObjective += s.score;
      else totalSubjective += s.score;
    }
    totalObjective = roundScore(totalObjective);
    totalSubjective = roundScore(totalSubjective);
    const newTotal = roundScore(totalObjective + totalSubjective);

    await tx.run(`
      UPDATE student_scores SET objective_score = ?, subjective_score = ?, total_score = ?,
        manually_modified = 1, modified_by = ?, modified_at = ?
      WHERE exam_id = ? AND student_id = ?
    `, totalObjective, totalSubjective, newTotal, userId, now, examId, studentId);

    // P1-8: 排名重算在事务内执行，确保数据一致性
    await recomputeExamRankings(tx, examId);
  });
~~~~
