## 36. Packaged systemd service runs as root with public data modes

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/01e622650ec88191aa464fa5fcccd97d?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=2)

关闭
使用 Codex 修复
Packaged systemd service runs as root with public data modes
严重级别
Low

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
e5f0253
5:33 PM Jun 27, 2026

操作者：Toyama Kasumi

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

This commit introduces the vulnerable deployment configuration by generating and including project-x-server.service in the Ubuntu server archive. Exploitation requires the operator to install the supplied unit without separately overriding its user or filesystem permissions; that is the unit's default and expected system-level behavior.

The generated systemd unit omits User, Group, UMask, and filesystem-hardening directives. A system-level systemd service therefore runs as root with the default 0022 umask. On a clean installation, the application creates /var/lib/project-x and its subdirectories without explicit modes, and SQLite and uploaded files are consequently normally created as root-owned but world-readable directories/files (0755/0644). The database contains student records, scores, reusable initial passwords, and provider API keys, while the answer-card directory contains uploaded papers and answer images. Any unprivileged local account can therefore read these assets under the default configuration. Running the network- and upload-facing Node process as root also turns a future parser or application code-execution flaw into host-root compromise. The packaged unit should instead declare a dedicated User and Group, set UMask=0077, provision private state directories (for example with StateDirectory and StateDirectoryMode=0700), and add systemd hardening such as NoNewPrivileges and ProtectSystem.

验证
 The packaged Ubuntu artifact contains an unsafe system-level unit and deployment instructions install/use it without compensating controls. The artifact does contain the unsafe unit, but its generated README documents foreground ./start.sh and does not provide systemd installation instructions.
 Under systemd/default process semantics, the unit runs as root with umask 0022, while application state is created without restrictive explicit modes; this was confirmed by systemd-analyze and source tracing.
 A focused realistic reproduction shows the resulting database and answer-card paths are traversable and sensitive files readable by an unrelated unprivileged local UID.
 Repository and dynamic evidence confirm that exposed storage contains student identity, reusable initial passwords, provider API keys, and uploaded assets, with no startup chmod, ACL, or encryption control preventing disclosure at this commit.
 Preconditions and impact are bounded to operators who install the supplied unit without overrides; root execution is treated as impact amplification rather than a demonstrated standalone RCE.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The generated systemd service specifies root-owned /var/lib paths and starts Node without User, Group, UMask, or sandboxing directives, so a system service defaults to root and umask 0022.
- The unsafe unit is written into the newly introduced distributable Ubuntu package.
- Answer-card, asset, and layout directories under the systemd-configured data directory are also created without restrictive modes.
- The application creates the database parent directory and database without restrictive explicit modes, inheriting the service's default umask.
- The affected database stores initial account passwords in addition to password hashes, increasing the confidentiality impact of local database disclosure.
- Provider API keys are stored directly in the same database and returned from it, demonstrating another high-value secret exposed if the database is locally readable.

~~~~typescript
export function getDatabase(): Database.Database {
  if (!dbInstance) {
    const dbPath = resolveProjectDbPath();
    mkdirSync(path.dirname(dbPath), { recursive: true });
    dbInstance = new Database(dbPath);
    dbInstance.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
    dbInstance.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
    dbInstance.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL");
    dbInstance.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");
~~~~

攻击路径分析

The original medium rating is reduced after separating impact from reachability. Impact is high because a successful local read exposes installation-wide educational records, reusable initial passwords, provider credentials, and uploaded images. Likelihood is low because network scope is none and exploitation requires both optional systemd-unit installation and an unrelated lower-privileged local account; the package README instead documents foreground startup and supplies no evidence that untrusted users receive server shell access. The executable PoC proves the permissions and disclosure once those plausible conditions hold, so the issue is not ignored as unachievable or self-only. Applying the required matrix mechanically, high impact multiplied by low likelihood yields low severity. No additional hard suppression applies because the attacker need not be root, operator, developer, or the owner of the disclosed records.

路径
Packaged systemd unit installed system-wide --Unit has no User, Group, or UMask directive--> Node service runs as root with default 0022 umask --Root process initializes SQLite and answer-card storage--> Application creates /var/lib/project-x state without explicit modes --Inherited modes make state world-readable--> Unprivileged local account reads 0755 directories and 0644 files --Direct filesystem and SQLite reads bypass application authentication--> Sensitive school records, credentials, API keys, and uploaded images disclosed

The Ubuntu package generator includes a systemd unit for the production web server. The unit starts Node from /opt, points sensitive SQLite and answer-card state at /var/lib/project-x, but declares no User, Group, UMask, StateDirectory, NoNewPrivileges, or filesystem protection. System services consequently run as root unless overridden. Application initialization creates the database parent and answer-card directories without explicit modes, and the upload path does the same for per-card directories. Under the ordinary 0022 mask, clean-install directories and files become 0755 and 0644. Application authentication and loopback binding do not protect direct local filesystem reads. The supplied executable validation reproduced root-owned modes and successfully read a student initial password, provider key, and uploaded asset as the unrelated nobody UID. The strongest counterevidence is deployment prevalence: the generated README instructs users to run ./start.sh in the foreground and never instructs them to install the bundled unit. It also does not establish untrusted local accounts on production hosts. Therefore the direct confidentiality impact is high when reached, but the repository-supported likelihood is low. The calibrated and policy-adjusted severity is low rather than the original medium.

发生可能性
Low - The unsafe unit is genuinely shipped and normal systemd semantics make exploitation straightforward once it is enabled, as confirmed by the supplied executable validation. However, the attack is not network-reachable: it requires a distinct unprivileged local account, installation of an optional unit that the README does not tell operators to install, use of its SQLite paths, and absence of secure overrides or private pre-provisioning. The repository does not establish that school server hosts normally expose local accounts to untrusted users. These constraints make likelihood low but not unachievable.
影响程度
High - Successful exploitation permits broad read access to the service's SQLite database and uploaded answer-card state. Repository evidence shows the database contains student identity information, scores and rankings, reusable plaintext initial passwords, and plaintext provider API keys. Uploaded papers or answer images can also be read. This is a high confidentiality impact across the installation. Unprivileged modification was not demonstrated, and root-process compromise is not counted because the repository evidence does not establish a code-execution primitive.
假设条件
An operator installs and enables the packaged project-x-server.service as a system-level unit without adding a User, Group, UMask, ACL, or other override.
The service uses the unit's SQLite configuration and fixed /var/lib/project-x state paths.
The state directory is created on a clean installation rather than being pre-provisioned with restrictive modes.
The host has an unrelated lower-privileged local account capable of ordinary filesystem reads.
The systemd manager uses the normal root service identity and default 0022 file-creation mask in the absence of explicit directives.
Installation and activation of the supplied systemd unit
No secure systemd override or pre-provisioned private state directory
SQLite-backed operation using the unit's configured paths
Access to an unprivileged local OS account on the server
控制措施
PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE=1 is set in the unit, but application authentication is bypassed by direct filesystem reads.
The server binds to loopback at 127.0.0.1:5174, reducing direct network exposure of the Node listener.
Root ownership prevents the demonstrated unprivileged account from modifying the files under ordinary DAC; the proven direct effect is confidentiality loss.
The README's foreground start workflow can be safe if an operator deliberately launches it as a dedicated unprivileged account with a restrictive umask and private state paths.
No User, Group, UMask, StateDirectory, StateDirectoryMode, NoNewPrivileges, ProtectSystem, or equivalent service hardening is present in the packaged unit.
No compensating chmod or application-level umask for the affected state was identified in the relevant repository paths.
盲点
The repository contains no host-account provisioning or SSH policy, so the prevalence of unrelated unprivileged local users on deployed school servers is unknown.
The repository does not contain an Nginx or tunnel deployment manifest proving whether the loopback service is publicly exposed; this does not affect the direct local-filesystem vector.
The README does not describe how or whether operators install the included systemd unit, so affected deployment prevalence is unknown.
Existing installations may have operator-supplied ACLs, secure unit overrides, private parent-directory modes, or a global systemd DefaultUMask that prevent disclosure.
The dynamic validation reproduced systemd-equivalent UID and umask behavior rather than launching the unit under a live systemd PID 1.
No independent application, upload-parser, or native-parser code-execution primitive was established, so host-root compromise remains an unscored amplification concern.

## 37. Score API exposes absolute scanner file paths

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/b4bf0da3256881919e1f6e8345eea83d?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=2)

e6ba585
5:38 PM Jun 19, 2026

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

This commit introduced an information disclosure by adding `sr.file_path` to the score API response without reducing it to a basename. The previous response exposed only scan record IDs and page numbers.

The changed score-details handler selects `scan_records.file_path`, aliases it as `fileName`, and returns it unchanged to API clients. These values originate from Multer’s `file.path` and therefore normally contain the absolute configured data directory, including deployment directories and potentially the operating-system account name. Any authenticated teacher with `exam:read` can obtain this metadata by requesting `/api/exams/:examId/student/:studentId/scores`; deployments with authentication enforcement disabled expose it without authentication. The disclosure is unnecessary because the grading-image handler immediately applies `path.basename()` and resolves the file beneath its own configured upload directory. The client additionally embeds the encoded absolute path in image request URLs, allowing it to appear in browser and reverse-proxy request logs. Return only `path.basename(file_path)` or the separately stored `file_name` field. This does not allow arbitrary file reads because the image-serving endpoint discards directory components, so the direct impact is limited to deployment-path disclosure.

验证
 Confirm the score-details route is production-mounted and serializes scan_records.file_path without basename conversion or redaction.
 Confirm file_path is populated from Multer's file.path and can contain the absolute configured deployment data path.
 Confirm the authorization boundary: enforced mode requires exam:read, a teacher can reach the route, students/anonymous callers are denied, and disabled enforcement permits anonymous access.
 Reproduce the disclosure through the real HTTP interface using a distinctive absolute path and assert the exact returned value.
 Confirm the client propagates the encoded path into an image URL and the image handler uses only its basename, bounding direct impact to metadata disclosure.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The client embeds the disclosed path into an image request URL, which can propagate it into browser and proxy request logs.
- The receiving image endpoint uses only `path.basename(req.params.fileName)`, confirming that exposing directory components is unnecessary.
- The changed query reads the raw `file_path` column and stores it unchanged as the externally named `fileName` value.
- The raw filesystem path is serialized into the HTTP response.

      recordId: s.recordId,
      fileName: s.fileName,
      pageNum: s.pageNum,
    })),
~~~~


攻击路径分析

Hard suppression does not apply: the effect is not self-only, the required scan records arise from normal product use, a lower-privileged teacher can trigger it under recommended authentication, and the repository's default compatibility mode can make it anonymous. Loopback binding is mitigated by the documented public Nginx deployment path, while enabling PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE removes the anonymous path but not teacher access. Calibrated impact is low because only filesystem metadata is disclosed and path.basename prevents arbitrary file access. Calibrated likelihood is high because the route is remotely usable in a supported deployment, requires only a normal GET with valid IDs, and was exercised successfully through the production Express interface. Applying the required matrix mechanically, low impact with any non-ignore likelihood results in low adjusted criticality.

路径
Remote teacher, or anonymous caller when authentication enforcement is disabled --connects remotely in a documented public deployment--> Documented public HTTPS/Nginx ingress to loopback Express service --forwards the API request to port 5174--> GET /api/exams/:examId/student/:studentId/scores --passes through the /api/exams gateway--> examGate checks only exam:read when enforcement is enabled --teacher exam:read is sufficient, or the default compatibility flag bypasses enforcement--> scan_records.file_path selected as fileName --raw database value is serialized without transformation--> Absolute path returned in JSON and encoded into grading-image URL --client submits the encoded path to the image route--> Image endpoint reduces the supplied value to path.basename

This is a real, low-impact information-disclosure vulnerability rather than a correctness-only bug. Multer writes scanner files under an absolute dataDir, the grading workflow persists file.path as scan_records.file_path, and the changed score-details handler returns that raw value as scans[].fileName. An executable validation using the production Express application obtained /srv/project-x/private-data/recognition/uploads/12345678/scan_secret.png unchanged: a default teacher succeeded in enforced mode, while an unauthenticated request succeeded when the repository's default compatibility setting disabled enforcement. The strongest counterevidence is that startServer binds only to 127.0.0.1 and the deployment guide tells operators to enable PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE=1. This is not dispositive because the same guide explicitly recommends public HTTPS Nginx proxying to the loopback port, and enforced mode still authorizes ordinary teachers with exam:read. The strongest impact counterevidence is dispositive for severity: the image-serving route applies path.basename and joins beneath its own upload directory, so neither arbitrary file reading nor traversal was demonstrated. Teachers are also expected to access the underlying answer images, although they do not need the server's directory structure. Preconditions are normal scanner and grading workflow state rather than operator-only or unrealistic setup. The result is reportable as bounded host-path metadata leakage, with possible secondary request-log propagation but no proven credential, file-content, integrity, or availability impact.

发生可能性
High - The path is returned by a normal production GET request with no user interaction or race. Scanner-created records are ordinary workflow data, default teachers possess the required permission in enforced mode, and the default compatibility configuration permits unauthenticated business-route access. The repository documents a public reverse-proxy deployment, and executable validation confirmed both the teacher and authentication-disabled paths. Likelihood is therefore high where qualifying scan records exist, despite deployment-dependent network exposure. | Remote network vector
影响程度
Low - The attacker learns server filesystem and deployment metadata and may cause it to appear in request logs. No secret value, file contents, score modification, privilege escalation, code execution, or availability effect is established. The downstream path.basename control specifically prevents using the disclosed absolute path for arbitrary file reads.
假设条件
The documented public HTTPS deployment through Nginx represents a plausible production configuration; this does not assert that every installation is publicly reachable.
Scanner ingestion normally creates scan_records rows with non-null file_path values beneath the absolute ANSWER_CARD_DATA_DIR.
Reverse-proxy request logging is deployment-dependent; the direct JSON disclosure does not depend on such logging.
A matching exam, associated card, student, scan batch, and scan_records row with a non-null file_path
Knowledge of valid examId and studentId values
A teacher session with exam:read when PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE is enabled; no session is required when the compatibility default leaves enforcement disabled
Network access to the Express API, directly or through the documented HTTPS reverse proxy
控制措施
optionalAuth parses supplied identities before API routing.
PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE=1 causes anonymous requests to receive 401 and callers lacking exam:read to receive 403.
The default student role lacks exam:read; executable validation confirmed a student was denied in enforced mode.
The handler rejects non-numeric examId and studentId values and requires existing exam, card, and student records.
Rows with null file_path are filtered from the response.
The grading-image handler applies path.basename and joins beneath dataDir/recognition/uploads/:cardId, preventing the disclosed directory components from selecting an arbitrary file.
startServer binds the Node service to 127.0.0.1, although documented public reverse proxying makes it remotely reachable in supported deployments.
No shell execution, dynamic import, or other executable sink consumes the disclosed path.
盲点
The repository documents public ingress but does not establish the configuration of any particular deployed installation.
No deployed reverse proxy was inspected, so actual request-target logging and retention remain unknown.
Full native OCR ingestion was not executed; the Multer-to-database origin of file_path is established statically, while the production score API and image handler were exercised.
The exact operating-system account name or other sensitive directory component depends on each operator's ANSWER_CARD_DATA_DIR and working directory.
The analysis does not claim that the disclosed path can be used for traversal or arbitrary file reading.

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

One security-related information-disclosure bug was introduced. Previously, the response exposed only the numeric scan record ID; the commit newly returns `scan_records.file_path` without reducing it to a basename.

The normal grading flow stores Multer's `file.path` in `scan_records.file_path`; the upload destination is derived from the absolute `dataDir`. This commit aliases that database field directly as `fileName` and serializes it in the response from `GET /api/exams/:examId/student/:studentId/scores`. Consequently, an authenticated teacher with exam-read permission receives values such as `/workspace/Project-X/data/answer-card/recognition/uploads/<card>/scan_....png`. The client additionally URL-encodes the entire path into grading-image requests, causing the path to appear in browser, reverse-proxy, and access logs. The grading-image handler only needs the basename, so the API should apply `path.basename()` server-side and return only that basename or an opaque image URL. The impact is limited to filesystem/deployment metadata; this does not by itself permit arbitrary file reads because the image handler reconstructs a path under the fixed recognition upload directory.

验证
 Confirm the production route and authorization permit an authenticated teacher with exam-read access to invoke the score endpoint.
 Confirm the normal upload/persistence flow stores an absolute, deployment-revealing Multer path in scan_records.file_path.
 Confirm the endpoint selects and serializes file_path verbatim as scans[].fileName without basename normalization.
 Reproduce through a focused realistic Express/API harness with an authorized teacher and observe the absolute path in the response.
 Confirm the client embeds the full value in a request URL while the image handler confines file resolution to the upload directory, supporting limited metadata-disclosure impact.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The client URL-encodes the disclosed absolute path into an image request URL, which can propagate it into request logs.
- The normal persistence path stores Multer's actual upload path in `scan_records.file_path`, confirming that the newly returned value is a filesystem path rather than merely a filename.
- The changed query selects the raw `scan_records.file_path` column as `fileName` without applying basename normalization.
- The raw path is newly serialized into the network response as `scans[].fileName`.

### [src/server/routes/score-editing.ts:189](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/6d424a30da7e6480f2c1a421569e4e72bd73dd99/src/server/routes/score-editing.ts#L189)

~~~~typescript
    scans: scans.map((s) => ({
      recordId: s.recordId,
      fileName: s.fileName,
      pageNum: s.pageNum,
    })),
~~~~


攻击路径分析

Technical calibration is low impact multiplied by high likelihood. Applying the required matrix mechanically, impact=low yields low for every non-ignore likelihood. Hard suppression does not apply: the effect is not self-only, does not require host/operator/developer/physical access, and does not depend on an attacker writing a protected database field. Although a teacher is an authenticated application user, the teacher is an explicitly modeled partially trusted attacker and is not authorized merely by exam:read to receive host filesystem layout; the server-to-client confidentiality boundary crossing is the vulnerability itself. The strongest counterevidence—loopback binding, production RBAC, and basename-based image resolution—reduces exposure or bounds impact but does not eliminate the verified response disclosure. The finding therefore remains reportable at low severity rather than being escalated or ignored.

路径
Authenticated teacher with exam:read --invokes with exam and student IDs--> GET /api/exams/:examId/student/:studentId/scores --queries sr.file_path--> scan_records.file_path containing Multer file.path --serializes verbatim as fileName--> JSON scans[].fileName containing an absolute path --client applies encodeURIComponent and constructs image URL--> Encoded grading-image request URL --reveals path to the teacher and potentially request logs--> Filesystem and deployment metadata disclosure

Attacker story: a malicious or compromised teacher uses the normal score-correction workflow and requests an existing student's score details. The production RBAC gate permits the request because teachers have exam:read. Recognition uploads are written under an absolute dataDir, and the grading workflow persists Multer's file.path in scan_records.file_path. The affected route selects that column as fileName and returns it verbatim, so the teacher receives a value such as /workspace/Project-X/data/answer-card/recognition/uploads/<card>/scan_<generated>.png. The React client subsequently embeds the URL-encoded value into grading-image requests, potentially extending disclosure into request logs. This is a genuine server-to-client information disclosure, not merely a correctness defect, but it exposes only deployment and filesystem metadata—not credentials, key material, file contents, or score integrity. The image handler reduces the supplied value to a basename and resolves it beneath a fixed directory, so repository evidence does not support traversal, arbitrary file read, code execution, or privilege escalation. The supplied focused executable validation observed a 401 without authentication, a successful teacher login, the exact absolute database path in the real score response, and successful bounded image retrieval.

发生可能性
High - The operation is low complexity and requires only a normal authenticated teacher request for an exam/student with scan data. Default teachers possess exam:read, the UI automatically consumes the affected response, and the supplied focused executable validation reproduced the disclosure. A documented public reverse proxy can make the service remotely reachable. Likelihood is tempered by deployment-dependent public ingress, the production authentication requirement, and the need for an existing scan record. | Remote network vector
影响程度
Low - The verified effect is disclosure of an absolute upload path and generated filename to an authenticated teacher, with possible secondary exposure in request logs. This reveals server filesystem and deployment layout but no repository evidence shows disclosure of secrets, arbitrary file contents, broader student data than the score workflow already returns, code execution, integrity loss, or availability impact. The fixed-directory basename control specifically defeats traversal and arbitrary-file-read escalation.
假设条件
Production follows the repository guidance to enable PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE and expose the loopback service through an HTTPS reverse proxy.
The attacker already controls a valid teacher account with exam:read; no student-to-teacher or unauthenticated privilege escalation is claimed.
Whether the encoded image URL is retained in browser, reverse-proxy, or access logs depends on deployment-specific logging configuration.
The affected exam has an associated answer card and an existing scan record for the selected student.
A valid authenticated teacher session when PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE is enabled
The exam:read permission, granted to the default teacher role
Known or discoverable numeric exam and student identifiers
An exam linked to an answer card
A scan_records row produced for that exam and student
控制措施
optionalAuth parses identities before API routing
PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE can require authentication and exam:read on GET /api/exams routes
Default deployment binds the Express server to 127.0.0.1:5174
The score route validates numeric identifiers and checks that the exam, card, and student exist
Recognition filenames are server-generated
The grading-image route applies path.basename and resolves only beneath dataDir/recognition/uploads/:cardId
The grading-image response uses private caching
Scan records receive a default 30-day expiration time
盲点
No deployment manifest proves that a particular installation has configured the documented public Nginx ingress; public exposure is deployment-dependent.
The server binds directly to localhost, and repository code does not consume the HOST value shown in one deployment example; remote reach relies on a reverse proxy or equivalent tunnel.
The focused validation could not instantiate the complete createApp composition because the checkout lacked the declared expr-eval dependency imported by an unrelated service.
The executable validation seeded an absolute scan_records.file_path rather than invoking the native OCR pipeline, although the repository statically establishes the Multer file.path-to-database flow.
No repository artifact establishes whether a deployed reverse proxy or browser retains request targets, so secondary log persistence remains plausible rather than guaranteed.
The score-editing route does not visibly enforce exam-specific teacher scope, but this report does not claim broader score or image IDOR impact beyond the validated path disclosure.

提交
e60693f
9:43 PM Jun 15, 2026

操作者：xiaoxu66609

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

This commit introduced the vulnerability by adding and mounting two sponsor GET endpoints without mandatory authentication. The existing optional authentication middleware does not reject anonymous requests, and no later authorization gate applies to these routes.

The application applies only optionalAuth before mounting /api/sponsor. That middleware always continues when no token is supplied, and the sponsor router does not install authMiddleware or check req.user. The route is also mounted before the permission gateways, none of which cover /api/sponsor. Consequently, an Internet user can directly request GET /api/sponsor to enumerate enabled payment channels and then GET /api/sponsor/qr/:channelId to retrieve a configured payment QR code, even when PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE is enabled. This conflicts with the documented policy that the feature is for logged-in students, teachers, and administrators, while the frontend login check provides no protection against direct API requests. The current committed configuration contains no QR files, but deployments are explicitly expected to enable them through runtime configuration. This can disclose payment-account metadata or payee identity encoded in deployed QR codes and permits unauthenticated scraping or hotlinking. The impact is limited because no academic records, credentials, or mutable operations are exposed. The router should require authMiddleware; because image elements cannot set a bearer header, the client can fetch the image through authFetch and display a blob URL.

验证
 Confirm an unauthenticated request reaches the mounted sponsor router while PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE=1; demonstrated by sponsor 200 responses while /api/cards returned 401 in the same server process.
 Confirm neither app-level middleware nor sponsor handlers require req.user; traced the always-continuing optionalAuth, unguarded router, and later route-specific gates.
 Confirm an enabled configured channel allows anonymous retrieval of QR bytes; reproduced 200 image/png and matching source/response SHA-256 hashes.
 Confirm anonymous access conflicts with the documented audience; readus/SPONSOR-PAGE.md states the feature applies to logged-in students, teachers, and administrators.
 Confirm deployment preconditions and bounded impact; documentation explicitly supports runtime QR configuration, while the committed default has no QR files and external reachability depends on the supported proxy/tunnel deployment.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The feature's documented audience is all logged-in users, establishing that anonymous access is outside the intended policy.
- Only optional identity parsing precedes the newly mounted sponsor router; the route is mounted before and outside the later RBAC gateways.
- optionalAuth explicitly permits requests without a token, so the application-level middleware does not protect the sponsor routes.
- Both newly introduced handlers return sponsor configuration or the configured QR file without authMiddleware or any req.user check.

~~~~typescript
/**
 * 可选认证中间件：有 token 则解析挂载用户，无 token 也放行。
 * 用于在“未强制登录”阶段仍然记录 created_by / 区分匿名访问。
 */
export function optionalAuth(req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
  const token = extractToken(req);
  if (token) {
    attachUser(req, token);
  }
  next();
~~~~

~~~~typescript
/** GET /api/sponsor — 赞助页配置（收款码 URL 由服务端解析） */
router.get("/", async (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const config = await loadSponsorConfig();
    const channels = config.channels
      .filter((channel) => channel.enabled)
      .map((channel) => {
        const qrPath = channel.qrFile ? resolveQrPath(channel.qrFile) : null;
        return {
          id: channel.id,
          name: channel.name,
          enabled: channel.enabled,
          qrUrl: qrPath ? `/api/sponsor/qr/${encodeURIComponent(channel.id)}` : null
        };
      });

    res.json({
      title: config.title,
      description: config.description,
      channels
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Sponsor config error:", error);
    res.status(500).json({ message: "赞助配置加载失败" });
  }
});

/** GET /api/sponsor/qr/:channelId — 按渠道返回收款码图片 */
router.get("/qr/:channelId", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const channelId = req.params.channelId;
    const config = await loadSponsorConfig();
    const channel = config.channels.find((item) => item.id === channelId && item.enabled);
    if (!channel?.qrFile) {
      res.status(404).json({ message: "收款码未配置" });
      return;
    }

    const qrPath = resolveQrPath(channel.qrFile);
    if (!qrPath) {
      res.status(404).json({ message: "收款码文件不存在" });
      return;
    }

    res.sendFile(qrPath);
~~~~


攻击路径分析

The established facts produce low impact and high likelihood. Applying the required matrix mechanically, impact=low with any non-ignore likelihood yields low severity. Hard suppression does not apply: the impact is not self-only; the attacker needs no operator, developer, local, physical, or authenticated privilege; and the documented operator act of enabling a payment QR is ordinary population of application data rather than a privilege the attacker must obtain. The route is part of a real runtime product workflow, and the supplied production threat model provides a realistic remote path through a proxy or tunnel. Conversely, escalation above low is unwarranted because the stock checkout contains no QR image, the endpoint is read-only, path confinement blocks arbitrary file reads, and the potentially disclosed payment material is intended for all logged-in users. Final decision: reportable as a low-severity authentication-control regression, not ignored.

路径
Unauthenticated Internet attacker --Sends anonymous HTTP GET requests--> Deployment reverse proxy or Cloudflare tunnel --Forwards public deployment traffic to the loopback service--> Express API on loopback port 5174 --Routes /api traffic through optional identity parsing--> optionalAuth middleware --Continues without req.user when no token is supplied--> GET /api/sponsor --Returns enabled channel IDs and QR URLs--> GET /api/sponsor/qr/:channelId --Reads the configured fixed-directory image and returns it with res.sendFile--> Operator-configured sponsor metadata and QR file

Project-X's Express service mounts optionalAuth for every /api request and then mounts sponsorRoutes without authMiddleware. optionalAuth expressly permits requests without tokens, while the later RBAC gateways do not cover /api/sponsor. The metadata handler reveals enabled channel IDs, names, descriptions, and QR URLs; the QR handler uses an enabled channel to return the configured image with res.sendFile. The supplied executable validation started the real application with PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE=1: anonymous /api/cards was rejected with 401, while anonymous /api/sponsor and /api/sponsor/qr/validation returned 200, and the returned PNG matched the configured fixture byte-for-byte. This is a genuine missing-authentication vulnerability in a product workflow, not merely a frontend correctness issue, because direct API requests bypass the UI's logged-in-user gate. Counterevidence materially limits severity: the server binds to loopback, the stock sponsor configuration has qrFile set to null, filenames are confined to the sponsor QR directory, and the disclosed content is intended for all authenticated users rather than a narrow privileged audience. It remains reportable at low severity because documented production exposure and documented runtime QR enablement make the anonymous trust-boundary crossing realistic.

发生可能性
High - For a deployment exposed through the production reverse proxy or tunnel, exploitation requires only two ordinary GET requests, no authentication, no user interaction, and no complex guessing because the metadata endpoint enumerates channel IDs. The executable validation confirmed the complete path while enforced RBAC blocked a protected control route. An actual QR disclosure depends on documented operator configuration, and direct service binding is loopback-only, but both conditions are plausible normal deployment states rather than attacker-held privileges. Likelihood is therefore high within an exposed, QR-enabled deployment. | Remote network vector
影响程度
Low - The maximum demonstrated effect is read-only disclosure of enabled sponsor metadata and a deployment-configured payment QR, potentially including payee or payment-account identity encoded in the image. The route exposes no credentials, academic records, arbitrary filesystem content, executable sink, or state-changing operation. The material is also intended for broad distribution to every authenticated role. Impact is therefore low.
假设条件
The deployment follows the supplied production threat model and makes the loopback-bound Express service reachable through a reverse proxy or Cloudflare tunnel.
An operator may enable the documented sponsor feature by assigning an enabled channel a QR filename and placing the corresponding image under data/sponsor/qr/.
The documentation's statement that the feature applies to logged-in students, teachers, and administrators represents the intended access policy despite the validation checklist showing a bare local curl command.
The Express API must be reachable by the attacker, normally through the deployment reverse proxy or tunnel described in the supplied threat model.
Disclosure of an actual payment QR requires an enabled sponsor channel with qrFile configured and the referenced file present under data/sponsor/qr/.
No account, bearer token, cookie, user interaction, or privileged attacker action is required.
控制措施
PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE enables RBAC gates for selected business-route prefixes, but no such gate covers /api/sponsor.
authMiddleware can enforce a valid bearer or query token, but sponsorRoutes does not use it.
optionalAuth parses a supplied token but deliberately permits anonymous requests.
The frontend hides the application and account menu until a user is present, but this is not a server-side authorization control.
Only channels marked enabled are returned or served.
A QR response requires a configured qrFile and an existing file.
path.basename and the fixed data/sponsor/qr directory prevent the channel configuration from serving arbitrary paths.
The service directly listens on 127.0.0.1, reducing exposure unless deployment ingress forwards traffic to it.
There is no route-specific rate limit preventing anonymous enumeration, scraping, or hotlinking.
盲点
The repository does not contain a concrete reverse-proxy, Cloudflare tunnel, load-balancer, or firewall manifest establishing the exact exposure of every deployment.
The committed checkout contains no real payment QR, so the confidentiality of production QR contents and any encoded payee identifiers cannot be measured statically.
The bare local curl in the sponsor validation checklist creates unresolved intent ambiguity even though the same documentation limits the feature audience to logged-in users.
No production cache, CDN, proxy logging, or hotlink-protection configuration is available to determine whether anonymous QR responses could be retained or redistributed beyond the application.
The executable PoC used a startup-only bcrypt shim because the checkout's installed dependencies omitted bcrypt; the tested anonymous handlers and protected control did not exercise bcrypt.

## 40. Known bootstrap password enables administrator account takeover

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/655cb18f92548191affa125ebb8a62c7?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=2)

关闭
使用 Codex 修复
Known bootstrap password enables administrator account takeover
严重级别
Low

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
313f2fb
7:32 PM Jun 12, 2026

操作者：Toyama Kasumi

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

This commit introduces the vulnerability by adding automatic creation of a fixed-password administrator and mounting the corresponding network login endpoint. Replace the fixed password with a cryptographically random per-installation bootstrap secret delivered through a protected channel, and require a one-time password change before issuing a fully privileged session.

Every fresh database is initialized with an active `admin` account whose password is deterministically set to `admin123`. The server creates this account before listening and then publicly mounts `/api/auth/login`; submitting the known credentials returns a valid eight-hour bearer token associated with the wildcard administrator role. This revision has no first-run secret randomization, `must_change_password` state, password-change route, or forced-change check, so the credential remains usable until an operator modifies the database through some out-of-band mechanism. Because the loopback server may be exposed through the documented reverse-proxy or tunnel deployment, an unauthenticated network attacker can claim the bootstrap administrator account on a fresh or restored installation before the legitimate operator secures it.

验证
 A fresh database deterministically creates an active admin account with password admin123.
 Unauthenticated POST /api/auth/login is mounted and accepts the fixed credentials over the real HTTP interface.
 Successful authentication issues an eight-hour token without any forced initial-password-change state or route.
 The created account is assigned the administrator role whose stored permissions are ["*"].
 A currently mounted protected API accepting that token, and Internet reachability without an operator-supplied proxy/tunnel, were not demonstrated; /api/auth/me returned 401 because authMiddleware is not mounted.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- Creates the default administrator during every server startup and mounts the unauthenticated authentication router.
- Automatically creates an active administrator with the hard-coded `admin123` password on every database that lacks an `admin` row.
- Defines the administrator as a wildcard role and explicitly documents the fixed bootstrap credentials, while the schema has no forced-change state.
- The network login route accepts the known credentials and returns the resulting administrator bearer token.
- Successful password verification immediately creates an eight-hour session token without checking or enforcing an initial-password-change condition.

### [src/server/db/index.ts:86](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/313f2fb0d231f7af79921596814806ac5c36058c/src/server/db/index.ts#L86)

~~~~typescript
export async function ensureDefaultAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  const db = getDatabase();
  const existing = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?").get("admin");
  if (existing) {
    return;
  }

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword("admin123");
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, name, role_id, is_active)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run("admin", passwordHash, "系统管理员", 1, 1);
  console.log("[DB] Default admin created: username=admin, password=admin123");
  console.log("[DB] 请登录后立即修改默认密码");
~~~~

router.post("/login", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { identifier, password } = req.body;

    if (!identifier || !password) {
      res.status(400).json({ message: "请输入用户名和密码" });
      return;
    }

    const result = await authService.login(identifier, password);

    if (!result.success) {
      res.status(401).json({ message: result.message });
      return;

### [src/server/services/AuthService.ts:31](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/313f2fb0d231f7af79921596814806ac5c36058c/src/server/services/AuthService.ts#L31)

~~~~typescript
  async login(identifier: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResult> {
    // 查找用户（支持 username 或 student_number 或 email）
    let user = this.userRepo.findByUsername(identifier);

    if (!user && /^\d+$/.test(identifier)) {
      // 纯数字，尝试学号
      user = this.userRepo.findByStudentNumber(identifier);
    }

    if (!user) {
      return { success: false, message: "用户名或密码错误" };
    }

    // 验证密码
    const valid = await verifyPassword(password, user.password_hash);
    if (!valid) {
      return { success: false, message: "用户名或密码错误" };
    }

    // 更新最后登录时间
    this.userRepo.updateLastLogin(user.id);

    // 生成 token
    const token = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const expiresAt = Date.now() + TOKEN_EXPIRE_MS;
    tokenStore.set(token, { userId: user.id, expiresAt });

    // 清除敏感信息
    const { password_hash, ...safeUser } = user;

    return {
      success: true,
      user: safeUser,
      token,
      message: "登录成功"
    };
~~~~


攻击路径分析

The original critical rating assumed both Internet reachability and an immediately usable wildcard administrator session. Repository evidence confirms the fixed credential and successful admin-token issuance, but contradicts both severity-driving assumptions: src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts:446-455 restricts direct exposure to localhost, and no checked-in ingress exists; src/server/middleware/auth.ts:28-73 defines authorization controls that are never mounted, so the token has no demonstrated privileged consumer. The issue is retained rather than suppressed because the public login route is a real product workflow, the unauthenticated-to-admin identity boundary is verified by executable evidence, and operator forwarding remains plausible under the supplied threat model. Applying the required matrix mechanically, low impact combined with low likelihood yields low adjusted criticality.

路径
Unauthenticated caller with local or forwarded access --submits the known bootstrap identifier and password--> POST /api/auth/login --looks up the active account and verifies its bcrypt hash--> Active fixed-credential admin row created on a fresh database --returns an opaque token and admin identity--> Eight-hour role-1 administrator bearer token --attack chain stops because no mounted privileged route authenticates this token--> Privileged token-consuming API not present or mounted in this revision

Attacker story: when Project-X starts with a database lacking an admin row, it creates an active role-1 administrator using a deterministic credential that is also disclosed in repository documentation and startup logging. An unauthenticated caller who can reach the service can submit that credential to the publicly mounted login route. Password verification then produces a random bearer token valid for eight hours and returns an admin user object. The executable validation reproduced this identity transition. The strongest counterevidence is that the exact server listens only on 127.0.0.1, the README documents only local use, and no proxy, tunnel, ingress, or load balancer is checked in. More importantly, authMiddleware and requireRole are merely defined: they are not mounted on the application routes, and /api/auth/me itself returns 401 because req.user is never populated. Consequently, the token does not provide a demonstrated capability beyond the admin-labelled session in this revision; card and scanner routes are independently unauthenticated rather than unlocked by this token. These facts are dispositive against the original critical account-takeover impact, but not against the existence of a fixed bootstrap-credential vulnerability in a real product authentication workflow.

发生可能性
Low - Use of the credential is trivial and was reproduced, and a fresh installation naturally satisfies the missing-admin-row condition. Nevertheless, repository artifacts bind the service to loopback, use an ephemeral loopback port in Electron, document only local URLs, and provide no public ingress. Remote exploitation depends on external operator forwarding described by the supplied threat model but not evidenced at this commit. This makes likelihood low for the repository-default deployment.
影响程度
Low - The unauthenticated-to-admin identity transition is proven, but the only demonstrated effects are issuance of an admin-labelled in-memory token, disclosure of the non-password user object, and update of last-login state. No mounted endpoint was found that uses the token to expose protected data or perform administrator operations, and /api/auth/me does not authenticate successfully because its router lacks authMiddleware. Counting the independently unauthenticated card and scanner routes as consequences of this token would invent an unsupported chain. Realized impact at this commit is therefore low.
假设条件
The attacker acts while the server is running against a fresh or restored database that has no existing admin row.
Remote exploitation requires an operator-supplied reverse proxy, tunnel, or equivalent forwarding because this revision binds the server to 127.0.0.1 and contains no ingress or load-balancer configuration.
The supplied threat model's reverse-proxy or tunnel deployment is plausible, but it is not documented or implemented by artifacts at this exact commit.
The validation PoC substituted bcryptjs for the unavailable native bcrypt package; database initialization, routing, credential selection, role assignment, and token generation remained repository code.
The application is running.
The database lacks a user whose username is admin.
The attacker can reach the loopback listener locally or through operator-configured forwarding.
The default administrator credential has not been replaced through an out-of-band database operation.
控制措施
Passwords are stored using bcrypt with cost 10 rather than plaintext in the database.
Unknown users and incorrect passwords receive the same generic authentication error.
User lookup requires is_active = 1.
Default-admin creation stops if an admin row already exists.
Session tokens are generated from 32 random bytes and expire after eight hours.
The production entry point binds directly to 127.0.0.1.
Electron launches the server on an ephemeral loopback port with context isolation, Node integration disabled, and sandboxing enabled.
No login rate limit is implemented.
No persistent forced-password-change control is implemented.
Authentication and role middleware are not mounted on privileged product routes in this revision.
盲点
A reverse proxy or tunnel may exist outside the checkout; no deployment manifests establish or exclude such forwarding.
No protected downstream service consuming this token is present in the inspected revision; an external component sharing the in-memory token store would be unlikely but cannot be evaluated from absent artifacts.
The validation runtime used bcryptjs because the checkout lacked the declared native bcrypt binary, although the relevant API and hash behavior were compatible.
Static repository analysis cannot determine whether operators manually replace the bootstrap credential in deployed database files before exposing the service.
The broader absence of authentication on card and scanner routes is a separate issue and was not attributed to this fixed-credential attack path.

## 41. Reverting a scanner skin change is not persisted

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/40eaaef423848191ae26118ccd7fffe1?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=3)

提交
715f158
7:34 PM Aug 26, 2026

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

Introduced: the commit adds post-login skin controls without keeping the comparison baseline synchronized after PATCH requests. This causes recoverable account-preference inconsistency but does not cross an authorization boundary or affect sensitive examination data.

After login, `serverSkin` is derived from `user.themeSkin`, but a successful settings PATCH does not update or refresh that user object. For example, if the account initially uses `paper-edge`, selecting `flat` sends a PATCH and changes the database to `flat`. Selecting `paper-edge` again is then compared with the stale login-time `user.themeSkin === "paper-edge"`; `skinPatchDecision` returns `patch: false`, leaving the database at `flat` even though the UI and local storage show `paper-edge`. After logout or on another device, the supposedly reverted preference restores as `flat`. The exact range makes this reachable through the newly added post-login switchers. Track the last successfully requested/persisted skin, update the authenticated user after a successful PATCH, or refresh settings rather than always comparing against the immutable login snapshot.

验证
 Confirm that authenticated scanner pages expose controlled post-login skin changes through onSkinChange={setSkin}.
 Confirm that ScannerApp compares against the login-time user.themeSkin and neither mutates nor refreshes that object after a successful PATCH.
 Dynamically demonstrate that, after an intervening change, the production guard suppresses a return to the original login-time skin.
 Exercise the authenticated production settings endpoint and verify that the first change persists while the suppressed revert leaves the database inconsistent with visible/local state.
 Establish preconditions and impact scope, including that only the authenticated user's cosmetic preference is affected and no security trust boundary is crossed.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The normal-operation decision assumes `serverSkin` reflects the current persisted value, although ScannerApp continues supplying the stale login snapshot.
- Every change is compared against `user.themeSkin`, but the fire-and-forget PATCH never updates that value; reverting to the login-time skin is therefore incorrectly skipped.
- The newly wired authenticated CardSelectPage switcher makes repeated post-login skin changes—and thus the stale-baseline failure—reachable through the product UI.

攻击路径分析

Pre-policy calibration is low: mechanically, low impact remains low even with high trigger likelihood. The separate final policy pass then applies the mandatory self-only-impact suppression. Repository evidence shows that the server uses req.user.id at src/apps/answer-card/server/index.ts:774-784, validates themeSkin at lines 807-814, and updates only that same user's cosmetic fields at lines 831-835. No realistic lower-privileged attacker can affect another identity, sensitive examination data, authorization state, or service availability. The issue is therefore a confirmed correctness bug but is not security-reportable and is adjusted to ignore.

路径
Ordinary authenticated scanner user --selects alternate skin and later selects original skin--> Post-login SkinSwitcher --records explicit selection and calls onSkinChange--> ScannerApp skin state and session/local storage --effect compares current skin with stale authenticated-user snapshot--> skinPatchDecision using login-time user.themeSkin --allows first change but suppresses return to login-time value--> Authenticated PATCH /api/users/me/settings --first request updates only req.user.id using validated enum values--> Same user's theme_skin and ui_style database fields --database remains at intermediate skin--> Visible/local preference diverges from persisted preference

Attacker story: an ordinary authenticated user selects the alternate scanner skin and then selects the original login-time skin. The controlled switcher makes both actions reachable in normal product use. ScannerApp persists the first change, but its fire-and-forget request neither updates nor refreshes the authenticated user object. The next decision still compares against the login-time user.themeSkin, so the return to that value is incorrectly treated as already persisted and no request is sent. Executable validation against the production guard and authenticated Express route confirmed that the browser-visible value became paper-edge while the database remained flat. The strongest repository counterevidence is the server-side control at index.ts:774-814: authentication is required, the target is derived exclusively from req.user.id, and themeSkin is restricted to two non-executable values. This is dispositive against cross-user modification, authorization bypass, secret exposure, code execution, and examination-integrity impact, although it does not disprove the functional inconsistency. The existing verification script is counterevidence against simpler login-transition regressions, but it permits only one post-login switch and is therefore not dispositive against the demonstrated two-switch sequence.

发生可能性
High - The functional defect is easy to trigger through normal authenticated UI actions: select the alternate skin and then return to the login-time skin before refreshing the user context. The supplied executable PoC reproduced the state divergence through the production guard and authenticated settings endpoint. This high correctness-trigger likelihood does not create an attacker advantage.
影响程度
Low - The demonstrated effect is limited to a recoverable inconsistency in the initiating user's cosmetic theme preference. There is no cross-user write, privilege escalation, sensitive-data disclosure, examination-integrity change, code execution, or meaningful availability loss.
假设条件
Production authentication remains enabled as required by the supplied threat model.
The scanner web client can be exposed through the operator-managed reverse proxy or tunnel described by the supplied threat model, although no concrete ingress manifest is present in the checkout.
The first skin PATCH succeeds and the authenticated user context is not refreshed before the user selects the original login-time skin.
The attacker story concerns an ordinary authenticated user manipulating only that same user's theme preference.
A valid authenticated user session
The account's login-time skin is one of the two supported values
The user selects the other skin and the resulting PATCH succeeds
Without refreshing the authenticated user object, the same user selects the original login-time skin
控制措施
authMiddleware requires a valid session when PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE is enabled.
requirePasswordChangeCompleted blocks ordinary API use until mandatory initial password replacement is complete.
The PATCH target is taken from req.user.id; the request cannot select another user's ID.
themeSkin accepts only flat or paper-edge and maps to fixed ui_style values.
The sink is a parameterized database update with no executable interpretation.
CORS uses an origin allowlist and credentials are not exposed through a wildcard origin.
The Node service binds to 127.0.0.1; remote access depends on operator-managed ingress.
The settings mutation affects only cosmetic account fields and does not touch grades, scans, exams, permissions, credentials, or publication state.
盲点
No concrete reverse-proxy, load-balancer, or Cloudflare tunnel manifest is present in the checkout; remote exposure is based on the supplied threat model and the server's reverse-proxy-aware configuration.
The supplied PoC did not execute a full browser automation click-through, although it exercised the production decision function and authenticated production HTTP endpoint.
Deployment-specific modifications to authentication, frontend routing, or user-context refresh behavior outside this commit could change reachability, but none are evidenced in the repository.
Static repository analysis cannot verify the operator's actual PROJECTX_AUTH_ENFORCE, CORS, proxy, or HTTPS settings.

## 42. Demo import can silently modify a real answer card

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/eaefc6f1683c81919dd846200f51363c?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=3)

关闭
使用 Codex 修复
Demo import can silently modify a real answer card
严重级别
Informational

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
721fb40
6:57 PM Aug 6, 2026

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

Introduced. At the baseline revision, the plain INSERT raised a uniqueness error when a fixed demo card ID was already occupied, preventing the importer from touching that card. The new INSERT OR IGNORE suppresses the conflict and allows all subsequent seed operations to treat the pre-existing card as the demo card. The importer should check the insert result and only reuse an existing row after verifying is_demo = 1 and the expected demo identity; otherwise it should abort or allocate a collision-free card ID.

The demo importer uses fixed card IDs such as 88000001 and 88000002. This commit changed answer-card creation from INSERT to INSERT OR IGNORE but does not verify that an ignored row is actually demo-owned. If a production card already has one of those IDs, seedExam continues using that real card as the card for a demo exam. For cards with question data, ensureDemoObjectiveBlock then inserts a demo objective block and hard-coded answer keys into the existing card. For card 88000001, seedFillBlankDemo similarly inserts a demo subjective block, questions, and an image asset. The stale comment in seedFillBlankDemo still states that an occupied demo ID will make seedExam fail, but INSERT OR IGNORE now suppresses that failure. Cleanup only deletes answer_cards where is_demo = 1, so a colliding real card and the injected blocks can survive demo cleanup. This can silently alter the layout or answer metadata used by real examinations and subsequent grading. The operation requires an administrator with SYSTEM_MANAGE to invoke demo import, so this is primarily an integrity and administrative-safety defect rather than a privilege-boundary vulnerability.

验证
 Fixed demo answer-card IDs can collide with a non-demo answer_cards row, and INSERT OR IGNORE suppresses rather than rejects that collision.
 Demo import remains reachable through the production admin interface with USER_MANAGE and SYSTEM_MANAGE authorization and proceeds after the ignored insert.
 Subsequent production seeding links demo exams to the colliding real cards and inserts card-owned blocks, questions, answer keys, image metadata, and an asset.
 Demo cleanup does not remove the colliding non-demo cards or the injected child records/assets, leaving persistent integrity impact.
 A focused test reproduced the collision, mutation, cleanup persistence, and production repository visibility using the actual service and schema.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The newly added OR IGNORE suppresses collisions with existing real answer cards without checking ownership.
- Seeding continues after an ignored insertion, links the demo exam to the colliding card, and may add objective blocks and answer keys to it.
- The fill-blank seeder trusts card ID 88000001 and inserts a demo block into it; its comment incorrectly assumes an ID collision still fails earlier.

  function seedExam(spec: ExamSpec): number {
    insertCard.run(spec.cardId, spec.name, spec.subject, spec.examDate);
    const info = insertExam.run(spec.name, spec.cardId, grade.id, spec.subject, spec.examDate);
    const examId = Number(info.lastInsertRowid);
    examCount += 1;

    for (const [num, total] of Object.entries(spec.scores)) {
      if (total <= 0) continue;
      const sid = studentIdByNumber.get(num);
      if (sid) insertScore.run(examId, sid, total, total);
    }
    if (spec.withQuestions) seedQuestionScores(db, examId, spec.cardId, studentIdByNumber, spec.scores);
    return examId;

### [src/server/services/DemoDataService.ts:483](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/721fb40d005711cbd507accbfadc857f57cdbac9/src/server/services/DemoDataService.ts#L483)

~~~~typescript
function seedFillBlankDemo(db: Database.Database): void {
  // 卡片不存在时跳过（防御性检查；演示卡号被真实数据占用时 seedExam 的 INSERT 会先行报错）
  const card = db.prepare("SELECT id FROM answer_cards WHERE id = ?").get(FILL_BLANK_CARD_ID) as { id: string } | undefined;
  if (!card) return;

  const blockId = "fb-demo-1";
  db.prepare(`
    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subjective_blocks (id, card_id, sort_order, block_kind, title)
    VALUES (?, ?, 0, 'fill_blank', '填空题（演示）')
  `).run(blockId, FILL_BLANK_CARD_ID);
~~~~


攻击路径分析

Before policy suppression, the calibrated medium data-integrity impact combined with low likelihood maps mechanically to low severity. The final policy pass then applies the privileged-only/protected-write-path suppression: execution requires an authenticated account with both USER_MANAGE and SYSTEM_MANAGE, currently the wildcard administrator, and there is no demonstrated privilege-escalation delta. The operation is also explicitly initiated from a developer-mode administrative workflow and requires an unlikely SQLite card-ID collision. The executable PoC proves the bug and persistence, so this is not a false positive; however, it proves an administrator-induced integrity/correctness failure rather than a realistic unauthorized attacker path. Under the required policy, the final reportability decision is therefore ignore.

路径
Authenticated administrator with user:manage, system:manage, and wildcard permissions --explicitly invokes privileged developer-mode operation--> POST /api/db/import-demo on the Express answer-card service --route calls seedDemoData on SQLite--> seedDemoData inserts fixed answer-card IDs using INSERT OR IGNORE --insertion result and is_demo ownership are not checked--> Demo exam linkage and child block, key, question, image, and asset writes continue --cleanup deletes answer_cards only where is_demo=1--> clearDemoData preserves the non-demo parent and injected children --surviving child rows are loaded through normal repository queries--> CardRepository and score-editing workflows consume the modified card definition

Attacker story: a malicious or compromised highest-privileged administrator, or an administrator acting on a mistaken assurance that demo import cannot affect real data, invokes POST /api/db/import-demo on a SQLite installation containing a real card with a fixed demo ID. The importer suppresses the primary-key conflict, treats the retained real card as the demo card, and writes demo children beneath it. The supplied executable integration test reproduced objective answer keys under 88000002 and subjective questions plus an asset under 88000001; those rows remained after clearDemoData and were returned by CardRepository. This establishes a real data-integrity defect. However, no lower-privileged attacker can execute the vulnerable import: the route requires authentication, USER_MANAGE, and SYSTEM_MANAGE, currently satisfied by the wildcard administrator. Although a teacher may plausibly arrange a fixed-ID card through writable card APIs, teachers already have broad shared-card write capabilities, and a separate administrator still must run the importer. The standard create and import workflows generate a fresh eight-digit ID rather than preserving a chosen imported ID, making accidental collision uncommon. The UI also places the action under developer mode and requires confirmation. Accordingly, the issue is in-scope as an administrative integrity bug but does not provide a reportable privilege-escalation or unauthorized attack path under the required final policy.

发生可能性
Low - The endpoint may be remotely reachable through the deployment proxy, but exploitation still requires an SQLite installation, an exact fixed-ID collision, and a separate authenticated highest-privileged administrator intentionally invoking a developer-mode operation. Normal create and import paths generate fresh IDs, making accidental collision uncommon. A teacher could plausibly plant a chosen ID through writable card APIs, but already has broad shared-card write privileges and cannot trigger the vulnerable import without administrator cooperation. Likelihood is therefore low.
影响程度
Medium - The demonstrated impact is persistent modification of one or more colliding answer-card definitions, including hard-coded answer keys, subjective questions, image metadata, and assets. These definitions are read by normal product and score-editing paths and could alter card layout or grading metadata for examinations using the card. No direct score change was dynamically demonstrated, the affected scope is bounded to rare colliding cards, and administrators can identify and repair the records, so the impact is medium rather than high.
假设条件
The affected deployment uses SQLite; the route explicitly rejects MariaDB deployments.
A legitimate answer card with is_demo=0 already has one of the fixed demo IDs, particularly 88000001 or 88000002.
An authenticated administrator holding both user:manage and system:manage deliberately invokes the demo-data import.
The colliding card remains associated with a real examination or is otherwise consumed by normal card, grading, or score-editing workflows.
Remote reachability is deployment-dependent: the repository server binds to 127.0.0.1:5174, while the supplied threat model states that production commonly places it behind a reverse proxy or Cloudflare tunnel.
SQLite deployment
A pre-existing non-demo answer card whose ID exactly collides with a fixed demo ID such as 88000001 or 88000002
An authenticated user with both USER_MANAGE and SYSTEM_MANAGE; repository comments state that currently only the wildcard admin satisfies this
The privileged user invokes POST /api/db/import-demo
The colliding card is subsequently read or used by a real examination workflow
控制措施
authMiddleware requires a valid session when authentication enforcement is enabled.
The /api/db router requires USER_MANAGE.
POST /api/db/import-demo additionally requires SYSTEM_MANAGE.
The current default permission map grants the required permissions only to the wildcard admin role.
Forced-password-change middleware runs before the affected business route.
The route rejects MariaDB and is reachable only on SQLite deployments.
The UI places demo import under developer mode and presents a confirmation prompt.
The server binds directly to loopback, although a deployment reverse proxy can expose it remotely.
answer_cards.id is a primary key, but INSERT OR IGNORE neutralizes the collision failure that would otherwise stop the import.
Cleanup protects is_demo=0 parents from deletion, but this control does not prevent or remove child-row injection.
盲点
The supplied PoC invoked seedDemoData directly rather than replaying the HTTP request, although the HTTP route is a thin authenticated wrapper and its reachability and permissions are explicit in source.
The validation demonstrated persistent mutation and repository consumption but did not execute a full grading run that changed published student scores.
No deployment manifests in the reviewed checkout establish a specific public reverse proxy, hostname, or load balancer; remote exposure comes from the supplied production threat model.
Repository evidence does not establish how often real installations import demo data after production cards have been created or how often generated IDs collide with the fixed demo range.
A crafted PUT to a fixed card ID appears plausible for a teacher with GRADE_WRITE, but the analysis did not dynamically exercise that preparatory path, and it would not create a meaningful privilege gain because cards are intentionally shared and broadly writable by teachers.

## 43. Reviewer can cast duplicate votes on disputed answer crops

[原始报告](https://chatgpt.com/codex/cloud/security/findings/27eec6b2da8c8191b31f11c048bf597f?repo=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FDalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C%2FProject-X&sev=&page=3)

关闭
使用 Codex 修复
Reviewer can cast duplicate votes on disputed answer crops
严重级别
Informational

调整 以提高未来扫描的准确性

提交
df48a8e
11:56 PM Jul 22, 2026

操作者：火箭

代码仓库
Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X
举报
聊天
概要

Introduced. Before this range, any reviewer ID already present in `score_breakdown` was unconditionally rejected and submissions were capped at `reviewMode`. This commit makes both controls conditional on the crop not being disputed and adds two extra rounds, while failing to require a distinct reviewer or validate that the requester is the auto-assigned dispute reviewer.

The modified duplicate-review guard rejects a reviewer only when the crop is not in the `disputed` state. Once two reviews disagree, any previous reviewer can POST the same crop again, and the new `reviewMode + 2` allowance accepts the duplicate round without verifying that the requester is the newly assigned reviewer or designated arbitrator. The duplicate score is then included as an independent observation in `computeMultiReviewResult`. For example, with a threshold below 10, scores `[10, 0]` are disputed; the reviewer who submitted 0 can submit 0 again, producing `[10, 0, 0]`. The median-clustering algorithm treats the two zeroes as a majority, excludes 10 as an outlier, writes 0 to `question_scores`, and recomputes the student's total. This also bypasses a configured arbitrator because prior reviewers remain permitted while only the arbitrator is prohibited from initial-review submission. Exploitation requires a teacher account with `grade:write`, access to the exam, and a disputed multi-review crop. The manipulation is visible in review history and affects one crop per request, but it can be repeated across disputed papers and directly undermines independent grading integrity.

验证
 Confirm a reachable grade submission path allows an already-recorded reviewer to resubmit a disputed crop under realistic authorization/preconditions.
 Confirm no assignment, claim, arbitrator, or reviewer-identity control elsewhere restricts that resubmission to the intended auto-assigned/fallback reviewer.
 Reproduce the state transition [10,0] disputed → duplicate 0 → resolved final score 0 using the production HTTP endpoint and resolver.
 Confirm the resolved duplicate affects authoritative question_scores and student_scores, rather than only history or preview state.
 Establish practical preconditions and counterevidence: ordinary teacher permissions and configured-arbitrator bypass were demonstrated; audit visibility and the fresh-database review_round prerequisite gap were documented.
验证输出结果

### 证据注释（原文）

- The network-reachable submission endpoint requires ordinary grade-write permission but does not distinguish an auto-assigned dispute reviewer from a previous reviewer.
- The 3P+ resolver treats two identical submissions from the same reviewer as a median-adjacent majority, allowing `[10,0,0]` to resolve to 0 while excluding the independent score.
- The changed guard explicitly permits an existing reviewer to submit again when status is `disputed` and allows up to `reviewMode + 2` rounds without checking that the requester is the newly assigned reviewer.
- The duplicate submission is appended as another full review, passed to the majority calculation, and, once considered resolved, persisted to question scores and student totals.

router.post(
  "/exams/:examId/block-crops/:cropId/submit",
  requireExamAccess,
  requirePermission(PERMISSIONS.GRADE_WRITE),
  async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      const examId = Number(req.params.examId);
      const cropId = String(req.params.cropId ?? "").trim();
      if (!Number.isFinite(examId) || !cropId) {
        res.status(400).json({ message: "参数无效" });
        return;
      }

      const scores = req.body?.scores as ReviewSubmitScoreInput[] | undefined;
      if (!Array.isArray(scores) || scores.length === 0) {
        res.status(400).json({ message: "请提供分数数据" });
        return;
      }

      const status = typeof req.body?.status === "string" ? req.body.status.trim() : "reviewed";
      const result = await submitReviewCropScores({
        examId,
        cropId,
        scores,
        status,
        userId: req.user!.id
      });
~~~~

### [src/server/services/ArbitrationService.ts:64](https://github.com/Dalian-No-5-Middle-School-I-T-C/Project-X/blob/df48a8ea0fc4286336d22928450397ac60fbce77/src/server/services/ArbitrationService.ts#L64)

~~~~typescript
  // 尝试找"多数接近分数" → 排除少数偏离者
  // 方法：逐对检查差值的聚类
  // 选取中位数附近的分数群来判断
  const median = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)];
  const closeGroup: number[] = [];

  for (const s of sorted) {
    // 从最小分和最大分往中间看，找出差异在阈值内的组
    let isCloseToAny = false;
    for (const c of closeGroup) {
      if (Math.abs(s - c) <= disputeThreshold) {
        isCloseToAny = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (isCloseToAny) {
      closeGroup.push(s);
    }
  }
  // 如果 closeGroup 未形成（可能只有一两个），尝试另一种方式
  const closeToMedian: number[] = [];
  for (const s of sorted) {
    if (Math.abs(s - median) <= disputeThreshold) {
      closeToMedian.push(s);
    }
  }

  // 如果 closeToMedian 覆盖了大部分（≥2 个且大于等于一半），且不是全部
  if (closeToMedian.length >= 2 && closeToMedian.length < sorted.length) {
    const avg = closeToMedian.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / closeToMedian.length;
    const outliers = sorted.filter(s => !closeToMedian.includes(s));
    return {
      disputed: false,
      needsArbitration: false,
      finalScore: applyRounding(avg, rounding),
      reason: `取${closeToMedian.length}个接近分平均，排除异常分${outliers.join(",")}`
~~~~
